EXECUTIVE READING

先给结论，再进入源码

核心机制

内层发动机负责每一步，外层管家负责一步结束后判断要不要重试、压缩、换模型、继续目标或等待后台工作。

证据规模

23 条 finding / 23 处引用

主要边界

当前页面复用既有源码账本；迁移到自研前仍需做部署级验证

适用建设

内层发动机负责每一步，外层管家负责一步结束后判断要不要重试、压缩、换模型、继续目标或等待后台工作。

值得借鉴

  • 内层发动机负责每一步，外层管家负责一步结束后判断要不要重试、压缩、换模型、继续目标或等待后台工作。
  • 用户插话时，纯等待可以立刻停；正在改文件的工具不会粗暴半路杀死，而是完成到安全边界再让模型听新指令。
  • 先把所有施工单审核、改好并固化，再按“可并行/独占”排程，日志看到的参数就是实际执行参数。

需要警惕

  • 当前页面复用既有源码账本；迁移到自研前仍需做部署级验证

00 · METHOD

研究口径：不是 README 摘要

README POLICY

README 只定位入口和产品命名；实现结论必须回到运行时代码、契约或测试。

FACT POLICY

L1 运行代码优先，L2 类型/事件契约补足，L3/L4 交叉验证，L5 推断单独标记。

01
DIMENSION · ARCHITECTURE

架构与所有权

3 条可定位结论；每条按事实 → 白话 → 工程影响 → 源码摘录展开。

01
L1factarchitecture

01 · 核心是强化 Agent loop，产品层再叠加大型 Session maintenance 状态机

源码事实

packages/agent 处理模型、工具、steering 和 telemetry；产品 AgentSession 在 agent_end 后按优先级处理 yield、空 stop、goal compaction、异常 stop、stream stall、model fallback、todo、async wake 和 session_stop。

白话解释

内层发动机负责每一步，外层管家负责一步结束后判断要不要重试、压缩、换模型、继续目标或等待后台工作。

对自研 Harness 的含义

自治恢复能力强，但 session maintenance 已成为复杂调度器，修改顺序容易产生竞态。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 879–897
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 898–918
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

  895  	try {
  896  		await runInActiveSpan(invokeAgentSpan, () =>
  897  			runLoopBody(
  898  				currentContext,
  899  				newMessages,
  900  				config,
  901  				signal,
  902  				stream,
  903  				telemetry,
  904  				invokeAgentSpan,
  905  				stepCounter,
  906  				initialMessages,
  907  				streamFn,
  908  			),
  909  		);
  910  	} catch (err) {
  911  		caughtError = err;
  912  		throw err;
  913  	} finally {
  914  		finishInvokeAgentSpan(telemetry, invokeAgentSpan, {
  915  			stepCount: stepCounter.count,
  916  			errorObject: caughtError,
  917  		});
  918  	}
  919  }
  920
  921  interface StepCounter {
  922  	count: number;
  923  }
  924
  925  function isDeadlineExceeded(deadline: number | undefined): boolean {
  926  	return deadline !== undefined && Date.now() >= deadline;
  927  }
  928
  929  function endAgentStream(
  930  	stream: EventStream<AgentEvent, AgentMessage[]>,
  931  	newMessages: AgentMessage[],
  932  	telemetry: AgentTelemetry | undefined,
  933  	stepCount: number,
  934  ): void {
  935  	stream.push(buildAgentEndEvent(newMessages, telemetry, stepCount));
  936  	stream.end(newMessages);
  937  }
  938  function emitInputMessages(stream: EventStream<AgentEvent, AgentMessage[]>, messages: readonly AgentMessage[]): void {
  939  	for (const message of messages) {
  940  		stream.push({ type: "message_start", message });
  941  		stream.push({ type: "message_end", message });
  942  	}
  943  }
  944
  945  /**
  946   * Resolve aside entries at the moment the loop is about to inject them. Each entry
  947   * is either a ready {@link AgentMessage} or a sync thunk evaluated here so the
  948   * producer can make the final inject-or-drop decision (return null) against
  949   * up-to-the-injection state — e.g. dropping late diagnostics a newer edit
  950   * superseded. Kept sync so it can never stall the loop.
  951   */
  952  function resolveAsides(entries: AsideMessage[] | undefined): AgentMessage[] {
  953  	if (!entries || entries.length === 0) return [];
  954  	const out: AgentMessage[] = [];
  955  	for (const entry of entries) {
  956  		const message = typeof entry === "function" ? entry() : entry;
  957  		if (message) out.push(message);
  958  	}
  959  	return out;
  960  }
  961
  962  async function runLoopBody(
  963  	currentContext: AgentContext,
  964  	newMessages: AgentMessage[],
  965  	config: AgentLoopConfig,
  966  	signal: AbortSignal | undefined,
  967  	stream: EventStream<AgentEvent, AgentMessage[]>,
  968  	telemetry: AgentTelemetry | undefined,
  969  	invokeAgentSpan: Span | undefined,
  970  	stepCounter: StepCounter,
  971  	initialMessages: AgentMessage[],
  972  	streamFn?: StreamFn,
  973  ): Promise<void> {
  974  	let deadlineTimer: Timer | undefined;
  975  	if (config.deadline !== undefined) {
  976  		const deadlineAbortController = new AbortController();
  977  		const deadlineReason = new DOMException("Deadline exceeded", "TimeoutError");
  978  		const delay = config.deadline - Date.now();
  979  		if (delay <= 0) {
  980  			deadlineAbortController.abort(deadlineReason);
  981  		} else {
  982  			deadlineTimer = setTimeout(() => {
  983  				deadlineAbortController.abort(deadlineReason);
  984  			}, delay);
  985  		}
  986  		signal = signal ? AbortSignal.any([signal, deadlineAbortController.signal]) : deadlineAbortController.signal;
  987  	}
  988
  989  	const softRequirementState = config.softToolRequirementState ?? { escalations: 0 };

  879  	if (typeof intent === "function") return "omit";
  880  	if (intent === "optional" || intent === "omit") return intent;
  881  	return "require";

  883
  884  function extractIntent(args: Record<string, unknown>): { intent?: string; strippedArgs: Record<string, unknown> } {
  885  	const { [INTENT_FIELD]: intent, ...strippedArgs } = args;
  886  	if (typeof intent !== "string") {
  887  		return { strippedArgs };
  888  	}
  889  	const trimmed = intent.trim();
  890  	return { intent: trimmed.length > 0 ? trimmed : undefined, strippedArgs };
  891  }
  892
  893  /**
  894   * Main loop logic shared by agentLoop and agentLoopContinue.
  895   */
  896  async function runLoop(
  897  	currentContext: AgentContext,
  898  	newMessages: AgentMessage[],
  899  	config: AgentLoopConfig,
  900  	signal: AbortSignal | undefined,
  901  	stream: EventStream<AgentEvent, AgentMessage[]>,
  902  	streamFn?: StreamFn,
  903  	initialMessages: AgentMessage[] = [],
  904  ): Promise<void> {
  905  	const telemetry = resolveTelemetry(config.telemetry, config.sessionId);
  906  	const invokeAgentSpan = startInvokeAgentSpan(telemetry, config.model);
  907  	const stepCounter = { count: 0 };
  908  	let caughtError: unknown;
  909  	try {
  910  		await runInActiveSpan(invokeAgentSpan, () =>
  911  			runLoopBody(
  912  				currentContext,
  913  				newMessages,
  914  				config,
  915  				signal,
  916  				stream,
  917  				telemetry,
  918  				invokeAgentSpan,
  919  				stepCounter,
  920  				initialMessages,
  921  				streamFn,
  922  			),
  923  		);
  924  	} catch (err) {
  925  		caughtError = err;
  926  		throw err;
  927  	} finally {
  928  		finishInvokeAgentSpan(telemetry, invokeAgentSpan, {
  929  			stepCount: stepCounter.count,
  930  			errorObject: caughtError,
  931  		});
  932  	}
  933  }
  934
  935  interface StepCounter {
  936  	count: number;
  937  }
  938
  939  function isDeadlineExceeded(deadline: number | undefined): boolean {
  940  	return deadline !== undefined && Date.now() >= deadline;
  941  }
  942
  943  function endAgentStream(
  944  	stream: EventStream<AgentEvent, AgentMessage[]>,
  945  	newMessages: AgentMessage[],
  946  	telemetry: AgentTelemetry | undefined,
  947  	stepCount: number,
  948  ): void {
  949  	stream.push(buildAgentEndEvent(newMessages, telemetry, stepCount));
  950  	stream.end(newMessages);
  951  }
  952  function emitInputMessages(stream: EventStream<AgentEvent, AgentMessage[]>, messages: readonly AgentMessage[]): void {
  953  	for (const message of messages) {
  954  		stream.push({ type: "message_start", message });
  955  		stream.push({ type: "message_end", message });
  956  	}
  957  }
  958
  959  /**
  960   * Resolve aside entries at the moment the loop is about to inject them. Each entry
  961   * is either a ready {@link AgentMessage} or a sync thunk evaluated here so the
  962   * producer can make the final inject-or-drop decision (return null) against
  963   * up-to-the-injection state — e.g. dropping late diagnostics a newer edit
  964   * superseded. Kept sync so it can never stall the loop.
  965   */
  966  function resolveAsides(entries: AsideMessage[] | undefined): AgentMessage[] {
  967  	if (!entries || entries.length === 0) return [];
  968  	const out: AgentMessage[] = [];
  969  	try {
  970  		for (const entry of entries) {
  971  			const message = typeof entry === "function" ? entry() : entry;
  972  			if (message) out.push(message);
  973  		}
  974  	} catch (error) {
  975  		discardAsides(out, error instanceof Error ? error : new Error(String(error)));
  976  		throw error;
  977  	}
  978  	return out;
  979  }
  980
  981  function discardAsides(messages: readonly AgentMessage[], error: Error): void {
  982  	for (const message of messages) {
  983  		(message as CommittableAsideMessage)[ASIDE_MESSAGE_DISCARD]?.(error);
  984  	}
  985  }

  987  async function runLoopBody(
  988  	currentContext: AgentContext,
  989  	newMessages: AgentMessage[],
02
L1factarchitecture

02 · steering 不只在轮间排队，还能在工具执行中协作中断

源码事实

loop 每轮前和 stop boundary 轮询 steering/asides；工具批运行时同时使用 hard abort 与 cooperative steeringSignal，interruptible 等待工具会被 steer 打断，普通有副作用工具只收到软信号并在边界注入消息。

白话解释

用户插话时，纯等待可以立刻停；正在改文件的工具不会粗暴半路杀死，而是完成到安全边界再让模型听新指令。

对自研 Harness 的含义

中断语义按工具类型区分，明显优于统一 AbortController。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 999–1022
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 1023–1048
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

 1058  					endAgentStream(stream, newMessages, telemetry, stepCounter.count);
 1059  					return;
 1060  				}
 1061  				// Yield at the top of each iteration to prevent busy-wait when
 1062  				// the agent loop is executing tool calls back-to-back.
 1063  				await yieldIfDue();
 1064  				// Park at the turn boundary while the process-wide pause gate is
 1065  				// engaged (host /pause). An external abort releases the park so a
 1066  				// cancelled run still unwinds while everything else stays frozen.
 1067  				if (agentPauseGate.paused) await agentPauseGate.waitUntilResumed(signal);
 1068
 1069  				// Build the provider-bound context before opening the turn. Queue
 1070  				// messages are added now but their events remain deferred until
 1071  				// provider preparation either succeeds or opens an error turn.
 1072  				const turnMessages = messagesToEmit;
 1073  				messagesToEmit = [];
 1074  				if (pendingMessages.length > 0) {
 1075  					for (const message of pendingMessages) {
 1076  						currentContext.messages.push(message);
 1077  						newMessages.push(message);
 1078  						turnMessages.push(message);
 1079  						(message as CommittableAsideMessage)[ASIDE_MESSAGE_COMMIT]?.();
 1080  					}
 1081  					pendingMessages = [];
 1082  				}
 1083
 1084  				let preparedProviderCall: PreparedProviderCall;
 1085  				let gateResult: AgentPreModelCallResult;
 1086  				try {
 1087  					if (config.syncContextBeforeModelCall) {
 1088  						await config.syncContextBeforeModelCall(currentContext, signal);
 1089  					}
 1090
 1091  					if (!directiveResolvedForTurn) {
 1092  						const directive = signal?.aborted ? undefined : config.getToolChoice?.();
 1093  						const softReq = isSoftToolRequirement(directive) ? directive : undefined;
 1094  						hostToolChoice = directive === undefined || isSoftToolRequirement(directive) ? undefined : directive;
 1095  						softRequiredTool = softReq?.toolName;
 1096  						softSatisfies = softReq?.satisfies;
 1097  						const softRequirementId = softRequirementState.id;
 1098  						if (softReq !== undefined) {
 1099  							if (softReq.id !== softRequirementId) {
 1100  								softRequirementState.id = softReq.id;
 1101  								softRequirementState.forcedToolChoice = undefined;
 1102  								softRequirementState.escalations = 0;
 1103  								for (const reminder of softReq.reminder) {
 1104  									currentContext.messages.push(reminder);
 1105  									newMessages.push(reminder);
 1106  									turnMessages.push(reminder);
 1107  								}
 1108  							}
 1109  						} else {
 1110  							softRequirementState.id = undefined;
 1111  							softRequirementState.forcedToolChoice = undefined;
 1112  							softRequirementState.escalations = 0;
 1113  						}
 1114  						directiveResolvedForTurn = true;
 1115  					}
 1116
 1117  					preparedProviderCall = await prepareProviderCall(currentContext, config, signal);
 1118  					gateResult = (await config.beforeModelCall?.(preparedProviderCall.context, signal)) || undefined;
 1119  				} catch (error) {
03
L1factarchitecture

03 · 工具调度支持 shared/exclusive 并发和完整 pre-dispatch 改写

源码事实

tool calls 在流结束前完成参数校验与 beforeToolCall，改写后的参数回写 assistant snapshot；执行时 shared calls 并发，exclusive 会等待之前 exclusive 和所有 shared；afterToolCall 可规范化结果。

白话解释

先把所有施工单审核、改好并固化，再按“可并行/独占”排程，日志看到的参数就是实际执行参数。

对自研 Harness 的含义

既能并发提速，也能避免 edit/bash 等副作用互撞；第三方结果还在单一边界做防腐。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 2067–2132
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 2133–2200
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

 2087  function resolveToolForCall(
 2088  	tools: AgentTool<any>[] | undefined,
 2089  	toolCall: AgentToolCall,
 2090  	resolveFallbackTool: AgentLoopConfig["resolveFallbackTool"],
 2091  ): AgentTool<any> | undefined {
 2092  	// Tools emitted via OpenAI's custom-tool path (e.g. `apply_patch` on GPT-5)
 2093  	// come back under their wire-level name, which may differ from the
 2094  	// harness-internal `name`. Match on either, preferring `name` for
 2095  	// determinism if both somehow collide.
 2096  	return (
 2097  		tools?.find(t => t.name === toolCall.name) ??
 2098  		tools?.find(t => t.customWireName !== undefined && t.customWireName === toolCall.name) ??
 2099  		// Not in the advertised set: let the host route side-transport tools
 2100  		// (e.g. xd:// device mounts) called by their top-level name.
 2101  		resolveFallbackTool?.(toolCall.name)
 2102  	);
 2103  }

 2105  /**
 2106   * Pre-dispatch phase for every pending tool call on `assistantMessage`, run in
 2107   * call order: intent extraction, argument validation, and the `beforeToolCall`
 2108   * hook. A hook `args` revision is revalidated against the tool schema and
 2109   * written back to `toolCall.arguments`; run before `message_start`/`message_end`
 2110   * (the streamed path) that makes the revision the single source of truth —
 2111   * history, execution events, persistence, provider replay, concurrency
 2112   * scheduling, and `tool.execute` all agree. Failures are recorded per call and
 2113   * surfaced by `executeToolCalls` at the record's scheduled slot.
 2114   */
 2115  async function prepareToolCallDispatch(
 2116  	assistantMessage: AssistantMessage,
 2117  	context: AgentContext,
 2118  	config: AgentLoopConfig,
 2119  	signal: AbortSignal | undefined,
 2120  ): Promise<Map<string, PreparedToolCall>> {
 2121  	const { resolveFallbackTool, intentTracing, beforeToolCall } = config;
 2122  	const prepared = new Map<string, PreparedToolCall>();
 2123  	for (const toolCall of assistantMessage.content) {
 2124  		if (toolCall.type !== "toolCall") continue;
 2125  		if ((toolCall as CursorExecResolvedCarrier)[kCursorExecResolved] === true) continue;
 2126  		const tool = resolveToolForCall(context.tools, toolCall, resolveFallbackTool);
 2127  		const entry: PreparedToolCall = { tool, args: toolCall.arguments as Record<string, unknown> };
 2128  		prepared.set(toolCall.id, entry);
 2129  		let argsForExecution = toolCall.arguments as Record<string, unknown>;
 2130  		if (intentTracing) {
 2131  			const { intent, strippedArgs } = extractIntent(toolCall.arguments);
 2132  			argsForExecution = strippedArgs;
 2133  			if (intent) {
 2134  				toolCall.intent = intent;
 2135  			} else if (typeof tool?.intent === "function") {
 2136  				try {
 2137  					const derived = tool.intent(strippedArgs as never)?.trim();
 2138  					if (derived) {
 2139  						toolCall.intent = derived;
 2140  					}
 2141  				} catch {
 2142  					// intent function must never break tool execution
 2143  				}
 2144  			}
 2145  		}
 2146  		const validate = (args: Record<string, unknown>): Record<string, unknown> | undefined => {
 2147  			try {
 2148  				if (!tool) throw new Error(`Tool ${toolCall.name} not found`);
 2149  				return validateToolArguments(tool, { ...toolCall, arguments: args });
 2150  			} catch (validationError) {
 2151  				if (tool?.lenientArgValidation) {
 2152  					const fallback = { ...args };
 2153  					delete fallback.__parseError;
 2154  					delete fallback.__rawJson;
 2155  					return fallback;
 2156  				}
 2157  				entry.args = "__parseError" in args ? { __parseError: args.__parseError } : args;
 2158  				entry.validationErrorMessage =
 2159  					validationError instanceof Error ? validationError.message : String(validationError);
 2160  				return undefined;
 2161  			}
 2162  		};
 2163  		const effectiveArgs = validate(argsForExecution);
 2164  		if (effectiveArgs === undefined) continue;
 2165  		entry.args = effectiveArgs;
 2166  		if (!beforeToolCall || !tool) continue;
 2167  		let beforeResult: BeforeToolCallResult | undefined;
 2168  		try {
 2169  			beforeResult = await beforeToolCall(
 2170  				{ assistantMessage, toolCall, tool, args: effectiveArgs, context },
 2171  				signal,
 2172  			);
 2173  		} catch (e) {
 2174  			// Contract: a throwing hook surfaces as a tool-error result without
 2175  			// aborting the batch — rethrown inside the execution span in runTool.
 2176  			entry.prepareError = e;
 2177  			continue;
 2178  		}
 2179  		if (beforeResult?.block) {
 2180  			entry.blocked = true;
 2181  			entry.blockReason = beforeResult.reason;
 2182  			continue;
 2183  		}
 2184  		if (beforeResult?.args !== undefined) {
 2185  			// Revalidate: a hook revision is untrusted input to the tool schema.
 2186  			const revised = validate(beforeResult.args);
 2187  			if (revised === undefined) continue;
 2188  			// Bake the revision into the message itself. On the streamed path this
 2189  			// precedes every consumer snapshot, so there is exactly one version of
 2190  			// the call anywhere downstream.
 2191  			toolCall.arguments = beforeResult.args;
 2192  			entry.args = revised;
 2193  		}
 2194  	}
 2195  	return prepared;
 2196  }

 2260  			args: toolCall.arguments as Record<string, unknown>,
 2261  		};

 2263  		const interruptibleMode = tool?.interruptible;
 2264  		let interruptible = false;
 2265  		if (typeof interruptibleMode === "function") {
 2266  			try {
 2267  				// Resolved from the prepared (possibly hook-revised) args so an
 2268  				// argument-dependent policy governs the call that actually runs.
 2269  				interruptible = interruptibleMode(args);
 2270  			} catch {

 2135  /**
 2136   * Pre-dispatch phase for every pending tool call on `assistantMessage`, run in
 2137   * call order: intent extraction, argument validation, and the `beforeToolCall`
 2138   * hook. A hook `args` revision is revalidated against the tool schema and
 2139   * written back to `toolCall.arguments`; run before `message_start`/`message_end`
 2140   * (the streamed path) that makes the revision the single source of truth —
 2141   * history, execution events, persistence, provider replay, concurrency
 2142   * scheduling, and `tool.execute` all agree. Failures are recorded per call and
 2143   * surfaced by `executeToolCalls` at the record's scheduled slot.
 2144   */
 2145  async function prepareToolCallDispatch(
 2146  	assistantMessage: AssistantMessage,
 2147  	context: AgentContext,
 2148  	config: AgentLoopConfig,
 2149  	signal: AbortSignal | undefined,
 2150  ): Promise<Map<string, PreparedToolCall>> {
 2151  	const { resolveFallbackTool, intentTracing, beforeToolCall } = config;
 2152  	const prepared = new Map<string, PreparedToolCall>();
 2153  	for (const toolCall of assistantMessage.content) {
 2154  		if (toolCall.type !== "toolCall") continue;
 2155  		if ((toolCall as CursorExecResolvedCarrier)[kCursorExecResolved] === true) continue;
 2156  		const tool = resolveToolForCall(context.tools, toolCall, resolveFallbackTool);
 2157  		const entry: PreparedToolCall = { tool, args: toolCall.arguments as Record<string, unknown> };
 2158  		prepared.set(toolCall.id, entry);
 2159  		let argsForExecution = toolCall.arguments as Record<string, unknown>;
 2160  		if (intentTracing) {
 2161  			const { intent, strippedArgs } = extractIntent(toolCall.arguments);
 2162  			argsForExecution = strippedArgs;
 2163  			if (intent) {
 2164  				toolCall.intent = intent;
 2165  			} else if (typeof tool?.intent === "function") {
 2166  				try {
 2167  					const derived = tool.intent(strippedArgs as never)?.trim();
 2168  					if (derived) {
 2169  						toolCall.intent = derived;
 2170  					}
 2171  				} catch {
 2172  					// intent function must never break tool execution
 2173  				}
 2174  			}
 2175  		}
 2176  		const validate = (args: Record<string, unknown>): Record<string, unknown> | undefined => {
 2177  			try {
 2178  				if (!tool) throw new Error(`Tool ${toolCall.name} not found`);
 2179  				return validateToolArguments(tool, { ...toolCall, arguments: args });
 2180  			} catch (validationError) {
 2181  				if (tool?.lenientArgValidation) {
 2182  					const fallback = { ...args };
 2183  					delete fallback.__parseError;
 2184  					delete fallback.__rawJson;
 2185  					return fallback;
 2186  				}
 2187  				entry.args = "__parseError" in args ? { __parseError: args.__parseError } : args;
 2188  				entry.validationErrorMessage =
 2189  					validationError instanceof Error ? validationError.message : String(validationError);
 2190  				return undefined;
 2191  			}
 2192  		};
 2193  		const effectiveArgs = validate(argsForExecution);
 2194  		if (effectiveArgs === undefined) continue;
 2195  		entry.args = effectiveArgs;
 2196  		if (!beforeToolCall || !tool) continue;
 2197  		let beforeResult: BeforeToolCallResult | undefined;
 2198  		try {
 2199  			beforeResult = await beforeToolCall(
 2200  				{ assistantMessage, toolCall, tool, args: effectiveArgs, context },
 2201  				signal,
 2202  			);
 2203  		} catch (e) {
 2204  			// Contract: a throwing hook surfaces as a tool-error result without
 2205  			// aborting the batch — rethrown inside the execution span in runTool.
 2206  			entry.prepareError = e;
 2207  			continue;
 2208  		}
 2209  		if (beforeResult?.block) {
 2210  			entry.blocked = true;
 2211  			entry.blockReason = beforeResult.reason;
 2212  			continue;
 2213  		}
 2214  		if (beforeResult?.args !== undefined) {
 2215  			// Revalidate: a hook revision is untrusted input to the tool schema.
 2216  			const revised = validate(beforeResult.args);
 2217  			if (revised === undefined) continue;
 2218  			// Bake the revision into the message itself. On the streamed path this
 2219  			// precedes every consumer snapshot, so there is exactly one version of
 2220  			// the call anywhere downstream.
 2221  			toolCall.arguments = beforeResult.args;
 2222  			entry.args = revised;
 2223  		}
 2224  	}
 2225  	return prepared;
 2226  }
 2227  /**
 2228   * Execute tool calls from an assistant message.
 2229   */
 2230  async function executeToolCalls(
 2231  	currentContext: AgentContext,
 2232  	assistantMessage: AssistantMessage,
 2233  	signal: AbortSignal | undefined,
 2234  	stream: EventStream<AgentEvent, AgentMessage[]>,
 2235  	config: AgentLoopConfig,
 2236  	telemetry: AgentTelemetry | undefined,
 2237  	invokeAgentSpan: Span | undefined,
 2238  ): Promise<{ toolResults: ToolResultMessage[] }> {
 2239  	const tools = currentContext.tools;
 2240  	const {
 2241  		hasSteeringMessages,
 2242  		hasIrcInterrupts,
 2243  		interruptMode = "immediate",
 2244  		getToolContext,
 2245  		transformToolCallArguments,
 2246  		resolveFallbackTool,
 2247  		afterToolCall,
 2248  	} = config;
 2249  	type ToolCallContent = Extract<AssistantMessage["content"][number], { type: "toolCall" }>;
 2250  	// Defensive: the outer loop already filters exec-resolved blocks before
 2251  	// deciding to invoke `executeToolCalls`, but skip them here too so the
 2252  	// guarantee lives with the code that would re-run the tool.
 2253  	const toolCalls = assistantMessage.content.filter(
 2254  		(c): c is ToolCallContent =>
 2255  			c.type === "toolCall" && (c as CursorExecResolvedCarrier)[kCursorExecResolved] !== true,
 2256  	);
 2257  	const emittedToolResults: ToolResultMessage[] = [];
 2258  	const toolCallInfos = toolCalls.map(call => ({ id: call.id, name: call.name }));
 2259  	const batchId = `${assistantMessage.timestamp ?? Date.now()}_${toolCalls[0]?.id ?? "batch"}`;
 2260  	const shouldInterruptImmediately = interruptMode !== "wait";
 2261  	const steeringAbortController = new AbortController();
 2262  	const ircAbortController = new AbortController();
 2263  	// Cooperative channel: aborted when queued steering (or an interrupting
 2264  	// peer IRC) is detected mid-batch. Tools receive it via tool context
 2265  	// (`ctx.steeringSignal`) and MAY react — e.g. an auto-backgroundable bash
 2266  	// backgrounds itself so the message injects promptly — but it never kills
 2267  	// anything; ignoring it is always safe.
 2268  	const steeringSoftController = new AbortController();
 2269  	// Interruptible tools (pure waits: hub wait, vibe) observe steering +
 2270  	// external + IRC aborts. Every other tool sees ONLY the external signal:
 2271  	// neither queued steering nor a peer IRC ever hard-kills a partially
03
DIMENSION · MODELCONTEXT

模型与上下文

7 条可定位结论；每条按事实 → 白话 → 工程影响 → 源码摘录展开。

01
L1factmodelContext

01 · 原生 tool calling 之外还有多种 in-band 方言和 Harmony 泄漏修复

源码事实

loop 支持 glm、hermes、kimi、xml、anthropic、deepseek、harmony、qwen3、gemini、gemma、minimax 方言，在 system/history/stream 三处编码；另检测 GPT-5 Harmony 协议泄漏并尝试恢复 tool call。

白话解释

一些本地模型不会说标准函数调用，它会把工具协议写进文本再解析；对模型意外吐出的内部协议也有专门清洗和恢复。

对自研 Harness 的含义

小模型/非标准模型兼容面很广，但解析器是额外攻击面和回归矩阵。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 26–34
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 35–45
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

   93   * Cap on consecutive re-samples triggered by a non-terminal stop
   94   * (`stopDetails.type === "pause_turn"`) without an intervening tool call. Each
   95   * continuation is a full model request, so a backend that never stops pausing

  101   * Cap on consecutive forced escalations for a single soft tool requirement.
  102   * A forced `toolChoice` guarantees the call, so this is purely defensive: if a
  103   * model somehow never satisfies the requirement, give up forcing rather than
  104   * spin the loop. Reset whenever the requirement id changes or clears.
  105   */
  106  const MAX_SOFT_TOOL_ESCALATIONS = 3;
  107
  108  /**
  109   * Whether a hard `toolChoice` for a turn conflicts with a pending soft tool
  110   * requirement — i.e. forbids tools (`"none"`) or forces a *different* specific
  111   * tool. `"auto"`/`"required"`/`"any"` and a same-tool force still let the model
  112   * satisfy the requirement, so they do not conflict and the soft gate stays active.
  113   */
  114  function hardToolChoiceBlocks(choice: ToolChoice | undefined, requiredTool: string): boolean {
  115  	if (choice === undefined) return false;
  116  	if (typeof choice === "string") return choice === "none";

02
L1factmodelContext

02 · 模型目录和协议实现分离，Provider 覆盖极广

源码事实

catalog 维护 AIML、Alibaba、Bedrock、Anthropic、Azure、Cerebras、Cloudflare、Cursor、DeepSeek、Devin、Fireworks、Copilot、Google、Groq、HF、Kimi、LiteLLM、Moonshot、Ollama、OpenAI 等大量 provider；AI 包实现 Anthropic/OpenAI Responses/Completions/Codex、Gemini/Vertex、Bedrock、Cursor/Devin 等不同 transport。

白话解释

模型“有哪些”由目录管理，模型“怎么说话”由协议驱动管理，两者不是一张巨型 if/else。

对自研 Harness 的含义

适合作为多模型网关前端，但 catalog 更新、认证和协议正确性必须分开测试。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 1–32
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 33–66
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

03
L1factmodelContext

03 · 流式请求有首事件与空闲双 watchdog，并识别本地工具忙碌

源码事实

provider wrapper 分别设置 first-item 和 idle timeout；synthetic start 不算真实进展，本地 server-side tool bridge 忙碌时暂停误判，Google CLI 冷推理有独立 5 分钟首事件 floor。

白话解释

模型一直没开口和说到一半卡死是两种超时；如果它其实在调用本地工具，不会被错杀。

对自研 Harness 的含义

对长思考、多协议和 server-side tools 的可靠性处理很细。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 181–204
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 205–230
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

  181  const LAZY_STREAM_IDLE_TIMEOUT_ERROR = "Provider stream stalled while waiting for the next event";
  182  const LAZY_STREAM_FIRST_EVENT_TIMEOUT_ERROR = "Provider stream timed out while waiting for the first event";
  183
  184  function hasFinalResult(
  185  	source: AsyncIterable<AssistantMessageEvent>,
  186  ): source is AsyncIterable<AssistantMessageEvent> & { result(): Promise<AssistantMessage> } {
  187  	return typeof (source as { result?: unknown }).result === "function";
  188  }
  189
  190  /**
  191   * floor used when neither caller option nor env var pins a value. Generic env
  192   * vars (`PI_STREAM_FIRST_EVENT_TIMEOUT_MS`, `PI_STREAM_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS`) still
  193   * take precedence unless a provider opts into OpenAI-family idle flooring for
  194   * local backends that users historically tuned with `PI_OPENAI_STREAM_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS`.
  195   */
  196  interface LazyStreamLimits {
  197  	defaultFirstEventTimeoutMs?: number;
  198  	defaultIdleTimeoutMs?: number;
  199  	/**
  200  	 * The provider implementation already wraps its upstream transport with
  201  	 * stream timeouts. Keep the lazy loader from racing it with generic errors.
  202  	 */
  203  	providerHandlesStreamTimeouts?: boolean;
  204  	/**
  205  	 * Apply OpenAI-family idle timeout precedence in the lazy wrapper. Used by
  206  	 * local backends whose users historically tune slow prompt-processing gaps
  207  	 * with `PI_OPENAI_STREAM_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS`.
  208  	 */
  209  	openAIIdleEnvFloorsFirstEvent?: boolean;
  210  }
  211  /**
  212   * Cloud Code Assist (google-gemini-cli / google-antigravity) routinely takes
  213   * longer than the global 100s default to emit its first SSE event when serving
  214   * the heavier Gemini 3.x Pro tiers at high thinking levels. Bump the first-event
  215   * floor to five minutes so callers stop seeing spurious "stream timed out while
  216   * waiting for the first event" aborts on legitimate cold reasoning starts.
  217   * The steady-state idle watchdog stays on the global default since the upstream
  218   * emits thinking tokens frequently once it gets going.
  219   */
  220  const GOOGLE_GEMINI_CLI_LAZY_STREAM_LIMITS: LazyStreamLimits = {
  221  	defaultFirstEventTimeoutMs: 300_000,
  222  };
  223
  224  const PROVIDER_HANDLED_STREAM_TIMEOUTS: LazyStreamLimits = {
  225  	providerHandlesStreamTimeouts: true,
  226  };
  227
  228  const OPENAI_IDLE_FLOORED_LAZY_STREAM_LIMITS: LazyStreamLimits = {
  229  	openAIIdleEnvFloorsFirstEvent: true,
  230  };
  231
  232  function forwardStream<TApi extends Api>(
  233  	target: EventStreamImpl,
  234  	source: AsyncIterable<AssistantMessageEvent>,
  235  	model: Model<TApi>,
  236  	options: OptionsForApi<TApi>,
  237  	abortTracker: AbortSourceTracker,
  238  	limits?: LazyStreamLimits,
  239  ): void {
  240  	(async () => {
  241  		try {
  242  			const providerHandlesStreamTimeouts = limits?.providerHandlesStreamTimeouts === true;
  243  			const idleTimeoutMs = providerHandlesStreamTimeouts
  244  				? undefined
  245  				: (options.streamIdleTimeoutMs ??
  246  					(limits?.openAIIdleEnvFloorsFirstEvent
  247  						? getOpenAIStreamIdleTimeoutMs(limits.defaultIdleTimeoutMs)
  248  						: getStreamIdleTimeoutMs(limits?.defaultIdleTimeoutMs)));
  249  			const firstItemTimeoutMs = providerHandlesStreamTimeouts
  250  				? 0
  251  				: (options.streamFirstEventTimeoutMs ??
  252  					(limits?.openAIIdleEnvFloorsFirstEvent
  253  						? getOpenAIStreamFirstEventTimeoutMs(idleTimeoutMs, limits.defaultFirstEventTimeoutMs)
  254  						: getStreamFirstEventTimeoutMs(idleTimeoutMs, limits?.defaultFirstEventTimeoutMs)));
  255  			// Providers with a server-driven local tool bridge (e.g. the Cursor
  256  			// exec channel) mark their stream busy while a local tool runs; the
  257  			// watchdog must not read that silence as a provider stall (#4593).
  258  			const localWorkSource = source instanceof EventStreamImpl ? source : undefined;
  259  			const watchedSource = iterateWithIdleTimeout(source, {
  260  				idleTimeoutMs,
  261  				firstItemTimeoutMs,
  262  				errorMessage: LAZY_STREAM_IDLE_TIMEOUT_ERROR,
  263  				firstItemErrorMessage: LAZY_STREAM_FIRST_EVENT_TIMEOUT_ERROR,
  264  				onIdle: () => abortTracker.abortLocally(new AIError.StreamTimeoutError(LAZY_STREAM_IDLE_TIMEOUT_ERROR)),
  265  				onFirstItemTimeout: () =>
  266  					abortTracker.abortLocally(new AIError.StreamTimeoutError(LAZY_STREAM_FIRST_EVENT_TIMEOUT_ERROR)),
  267  				abortSignal: options.signal,
  268  				// The synthetic `start` event is yielded immediately by every provider before
  269  				// the upstream model has emitted any tokens. Treating it as the first "real"
  270  				// item would flip the watchdog from `firstItemTimeoutMs` to the much shorter
  271  				// `idleTimeoutMs` while we're still legitimately waiting on the model's
  272  				// first response (slow first-token from reasoning models, cold proxies, etc.).
  273  				isProgressItem: event => (event as AssistantMessageEvent).type !== "start",
  274  				hasPendingLocalWork: localWorkSource ? () => localWorkSource.hasPendingLocalWork : undefined,
  275  			});
  276
  277  			for await (const event of watchedSource) {
  278  				target.push(event);
  279  			}
  280  			if (hasFinalResult(source)) {
  281  				target.end(await source.result());
  282  			} else {
  283  				target.end();
  284  			}
  285  		} catch (error) {
  286  			const stopReason = abortTracker.wasCallerAbort() ? "aborted" : "error";
  287  			const message = createLazyLoadErrorMessage(model, error, stopReason);
  288  			target.push({ type: "error", reason: stopReason, error: message });
  289  			target.end(message);
  290  		}
  291  	})();
  292  }
  293
  294  function createLazyLoadErrorMessage<TApi extends Api>(
  295  	model: Model<TApi>,
  296  	error: unknown,
  297  	stopReason: Extract<AssistantMessage["stopReason"], "aborted" | "error"> = "error",
  298  ): AssistantMessage {
  299  	return {
  300  		role: "assistant",
  301  		content: [],

packages/ai/src/providers/register-builtins.ts · a53e4e790d39当前源码 ↗
  181
  182  const LAZY_STREAM_IDLE_TIMEOUT_ERROR = "Provider stream stalled while waiting for the next event";
  183  const LAZY_STREAM_FIRST_EVENT_TIMEOUT_ERROR = "Provider stream timed out while waiting for the first event";
  184
  185  function hasFinalResult(
  186  	source: AsyncIterable<AssistantMessageEvent>,
  187  ): source is AsyncIterable<AssistantMessageEvent> & { result(): Promise<AssistantMessage> } {
  188  	return typeof (source as { result?: unknown }).result === "function";
  189  }
  190
  191  /**
  192   * floor used when neither caller option nor env var pins a value. Generic env
  193   * vars (`PI_STREAM_FIRST_EVENT_TIMEOUT_MS`, `PI_STREAM_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS`) still
  194   * take precedence unless a provider opts into OpenAI-family idle flooring for
  195   * local backends that users historically tuned with `PI_OPENAI_STREAM_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS`.
  196   */
  197  interface LazyStreamLimits {
  198  	defaultFirstEventTimeoutMs?: number;
  199  	defaultIdleTimeoutMs?: number;
  200  	/**
  201  	 * The provider implementation already wraps its upstream transport with
  202  	 * stream timeouts. Keep the lazy loader from racing it with generic errors.
  203  	 */
  204  	providerHandlesStreamTimeouts?: boolean;
  205  	/**
  206  	 * The provider retries or fails over when no first event arrives, while the
  207  	 * lazy wrapper continues to own steady-state idle detection.
  208  	 */
  209  	providerHandlesFirstEventTimeouts?: boolean;
  210  	/**
  211  	 * Apply OpenAI-family idle timeout precedence in the lazy wrapper. Used by
  212  	 * local backends whose users historically tune slow prompt-processing gaps
  213  	 * with `PI_OPENAI_STREAM_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS`.
  214  	 */
  215  	openAIIdleEnvFloorsFirstEvent?: boolean;
  216  }
  217  /**
  218   * Cloud Code Assist owns first-event detection because Antigravity can return
  219   * successful headers and then never emit an SSE event. Keeping the watchdog in
  220   * the provider lets it fail over before surfacing an error; the lazy wrapper
  221   * still catches post-first-event stalls.
  222   */
  223  const GOOGLE_GEMINI_CLI_LAZY_STREAM_LIMITS: LazyStreamLimits = {
  224  	providerHandlesFirstEventTimeouts: true,
  225  };
  226
  227  const PROVIDER_HANDLED_STREAM_TIMEOUTS: LazyStreamLimits = {
  228  	providerHandlesStreamTimeouts: true,
  229  };
  230
  231  const OPENAI_IDLE_FLOORED_LAZY_STREAM_LIMITS: LazyStreamLimits = {
  232  	openAIIdleEnvFloorsFirstEvent: true,
  233  };
  234
  235  function forwardStream<TApi extends Api>(
  236  	target: EventStreamImpl,
  237  	source: AsyncIterable<AssistantMessageEvent>,
  238  	model: Model<TApi>,
  239  	options: OptionsForApi<TApi>,
  240  	abortTracker: AbortSourceTracker,
  241  	limits?: LazyStreamLimits,
  242  ): void {
  243  	(async () => {
  244  		try {
  245  			const providerHandlesStreamTimeouts = limits?.providerHandlesStreamTimeouts === true;
  246  			const providerHandlesFirstEventTimeouts = limits?.providerHandlesFirstEventTimeouts === true;
  247  			// Per-model catalog compat can widen the fallback watchdog for hosts
  248  			// with no keepalive events (e.g. Bedrock reasoning models that go
  249  			// quiet for minutes mid-thinking, issue #4758). Caller options and
  250  			// env overrides still take precedence over the compat fallback. The
  251  			// annotated local up-casts the generic CompatOf<TApi> by assignment,
  252  			// so any compat shape redeclaring `streamIdleTimeoutMs` with another
  253  			// type is a compile error here.
  254  			const compat: CompatOf<Api> | undefined = model.compat;
  255  			const compatIdleTimeoutMs =
  256  				compat !== undefined && "streamIdleTimeoutMs" in compat ? compat.streamIdleTimeoutMs : undefined;
  257  			const idleTimeoutFallbackMs = compatIdleTimeoutMs ?? limits?.defaultIdleTimeoutMs;
  258  			const idleTimeoutMs = providerHandlesStreamTimeouts
  259  				? undefined
  260  				: (options.streamIdleTimeoutMs ??
  261  					(limits?.openAIIdleEnvFloorsFirstEvent
  262  						? getOpenAIStreamIdleTimeoutMs(idleTimeoutFallbackMs)
  263  						: getStreamIdleTimeoutMs(idleTimeoutFallbackMs)));
  264  			const firstItemTimeoutMs =
  265  				providerHandlesStreamTimeouts || providerHandlesFirstEventTimeouts
  266  					? 0
  267  					: (options.streamFirstEventTimeoutMs ??
  268  						(limits?.openAIIdleEnvFloorsFirstEvent
  269  							? getOpenAIStreamFirstEventTimeoutMs(idleTimeoutMs, limits.defaultFirstEventTimeoutMs)
  270  							: getStreamFirstEventTimeoutMs(idleTimeoutMs, limits?.defaultFirstEventTimeoutMs)));
  271  			// Providers with a server-driven local tool bridge (e.g. the Cursor
  272  			// exec channel) mark their stream busy while a local tool runs; the
  273  			// watchdog must not read that silence as a provider stall (#4593).
  274  			const localWorkSource = source instanceof EventStreamImpl ? source : undefined;
  275  			const watchedSource = iterateWithIdleTimeout(source, {
  276  				idleTimeoutMs,
  277  				firstItemTimeoutMs,
  278  				errorMessage: LAZY_STREAM_IDLE_TIMEOUT_ERROR,
  279  				firstItemErrorMessage: LAZY_STREAM_FIRST_EVENT_TIMEOUT_ERROR,
  280  				onIdle: () => abortTracker.abortLocally(new AIError.StreamTimeoutError(LAZY_STREAM_IDLE_TIMEOUT_ERROR)),
  281  				onFirstItemTimeout: () =>
  282  					abortTracker.abortLocally(new AIError.StreamTimeoutError(LAZY_STREAM_FIRST_EVENT_TIMEOUT_ERROR)),
  283  				abortSignal: options.signal,
  284  				// The synthetic `start` event is yielded immediately by every provider before
  285  				// the upstream model has emitted any tokens. Treating it as the first "real"
  286  				// item would flip the watchdog from `firstItemTimeoutMs` to the much shorter
  287  				// `idleTimeoutMs` while we're still legitimately waiting on the model's
  288  				// first response (slow first-token from reasoning models, cold proxies, etc.).
  289  				isProgressItem: event => (event as AssistantMessageEvent).type !== "start",
  290  				hasPendingLocalWork: localWorkSource ? () => localWorkSource.hasPendingLocalWork : undefined,
  291  			});
  292
  293  			for await (const event of watchedSource) {
  294  				target.push(event);
  295  			}
  296  			if (hasFinalResult(source)) {
  297  				target.end(await source.result());
  298  			} else {
  299  				target.end();
  300  			}
  301  		} catch (error) {
04
L1factmodelContext

04 · 压缩不是单一摘要，而是 context-full/handoff/shake/snapcompact 多策略

源码事实

CompactionSettings 支持 context-full、handoff、shake、snapcompact、off；默认 reserve 同时取固定值和窗口 15% 的较大者，对小窗口再防止 reserve 不可能；threshold 可按 token 或百分比。

白话解释

可以选择传统摘要、交接文档、删除低价值块或 frame 化压缩；预算还会随模型窗口缩放。

对自研 Harness 的含义

策略实验空间很大，但同一会话跨模型/策略迁移要处理兼容性。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 148–167
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 168–189
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

  205  // ============================================================================

  207  // ============================================================================

  209  /**
  210   * Calculate total context tokens from usage.
  211   * Uses the native totalTokens field when available, falls back to computing from components.
  212   * Provider-side orchestration tokens are billable but never replay into the
  213   * conversation prefix, so they are excluded from context sizing to keep
  214   * auto-compaction and context-promotion thresholds honest.
  215   */
  216  export function calculateContextTokens(usage: Usage): number {
  217  	const orchestration = usage.orchestration;
  218  	const orchestrationTotal = orchestration
  219  		? (orchestration.input ?? 0) + (orchestration.output ?? 0) + (orchestration.cacheRead ?? 0)
  220  		: 0;
  221  	const raw = usage.totalTokens || usage.input + usage.output + usage.cacheRead + usage.cacheWrite;
  222  	return Math.max(0, raw - orchestrationTotal);
  223  }
  224
  225  export function calculatePromptTokens(usage: Usage): number {
  226  	const promptTokens = usage.input + usage.cacheRead + usage.cacheWrite;
  227  	if (promptTokens > 0) {
  228  		return promptTokens;
  229  	}
  230  	return calculateContextTokens(usage);
  231  }

  233  /**
  234   * Get usage from an assistant message if available.
  235   * Skips aborted and error messages as they don't have valid usage data.
  236   */
  237  function getAssistantUsage(msg: AgentMessage): Usage | undefined {
  238  	if (msg.role === "assistant" && "usage" in msg) {
  239  		const assistantMsg = msg as AssistantMessage;
  240  		if (assistantMsg.stopReason !== "aborted" && assistantMsg.stopReason !== "error" && assistantMsg.usage) {
  241  			return assistantMsg.usage;
  242  		}
  243  	}
  244  	return undefined;
  245  }
  246
  247  /**
  248   * Find the last non-aborted assistant message usage from session entries.
  249   */
  250  export function getLastAssistantUsage(entries: SessionEntry[]): Usage | undefined {
  251  	for (let i = entries.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
  252  		const entry = entries[i];
  253  		if (entry.type === "message") {
  254  			const usage = getAssistantUsage(entry.message);
  255  			if (usage) return usage;
  256  		}
  257  	}
  258  	return undefined;
  259  }

  191  /**
  192   * Hard ceiling on a generated compaction summary.
  193   *
  194   * The summary budget is `floor(0.8 * reserveTokens)`, and the effective reserve is
  195   * at least 15% of the declared context window, so a 1M-token window authorizes a
  196   * ~120k-token summary. At that size the model copies rather than compresses, and
  197   * output is the slowest and most expensive token class. Capping absolutely keeps
  198   * the compression ratio improving with window size instead of degrading. The value
  199   * mirrors {@link DEFAULT_RESERVE_TOKENS} so this adds no new tuning constant.
  200   */
  201  export const MAX_SUMMARY_TOKENS = DEFAULT_RESERVE_TOKENS;

  205  // chose a reserve" from "user explicitly configured the default value".

  223  ): boolean {
  224  	if (settings.remoteEnabled === false) return false;
  225  	return (
  226  		shouldUseOpenAiRemoteCompaction(model) ||
  227  		(settings.remoteStreamingV2Enabled !== false && shouldUseCompactionV2Streaming(model))

  259  	if (promptTokens > 0) {

05
L1factmodelContext

05 · 传统摘要保留近期原文、拆分超长 turn、迁移文件操作和旧 archive

源码事实

cut point 只选合法用户/branch/custom 边界，允许 split turn；历史和 turn prefix 可并行摘要，合并旧 summary，提取 read/modified files。旧 snapcompact archive 在换到不兼容模型时重新展开为本地可读摘要。

白话解释

远处历史压成结构化摘要，最近工作保留原文；一个回合过长就拆开。换模型时也不会把上一家模型才懂的压缩黑盒直接留下。

对自研 Harness 的含义

兼顾近期保真、文件状态和跨 provider 可移植性。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 501–567
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 568–636
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

叙述来自历史证据账本 · ed4c78bc0faa · 打开账本提交 ↗
packages/agent/src/compaction/compaction.ts:501–636固定提交 ↗
  501  	const cutPoints: number[] = [];
  502  	for (let i = startIndex; i < endIndex; i++) {
  503  		const entry = entries[i];
  504  		switch (entry.type) {
  505  			case "message": {
  506  				const role = entry.message.role as string;
  507  				switch (role) {
  508  					case "bashExecution":
  509  					case "hookMessage":
  510  					case "branchSummary":
  511  					case "compactionSummary":
  512  					case "user":
  513  					case "assistant":
  514  						cutPoints.push(i);
  515  						break;
  516  					case "toolResult":
  517  						break;
  518  				}
  519  				break;
  520  			}
  521  			case "thinking_level_change":
  522  			case "model_change":
  523  			case "compaction":
  524  			case "branch_summary":
  525  			case "custom":
  526  			case "custom_message":
  527  			case "label":
  528  		}
  529  		// branch_summary and custom_message are user-role messages, valid cut points
  530  		if (entry.type === "branch_summary" || entry.type === "custom_message") {
  531  			cutPoints.push(i);
  532  		}
  533  	}
  534  	return cutPoints;
  535  }
  536
  537  /**
  538   * Find the user message (or bashExecution) that starts the turn containing the given entry index.
  539   * Returns -1 if no turn start found before the index.
  540   * BashExecutionMessage is treated like a user message for turn boundaries.
  541   */
  542  export function findTurnStartIndex(entries: SessionEntry[], entryIndex: number, startIndex: number): number {
  543  	for (let i = entryIndex; i >= startIndex; i--) {
  544  		const entry = entries[i];
  545  		// branch_summary and custom_message are user-role messages, can start a turn
  546  		if (entry.type === "branch_summary" || entry.type === "custom_message") {
  547  			return i;
  548  		}
  549  		if (entry.type === "message") {
  550  			const role = entry.message.role as string;
  551  			if (role === "user" || role === "bashExecution") {
  552  				return i;
  553  			}
  554  		}
  555  	}
  556  	return -1;
  557  }
  558
  559  export interface CutPointResult {
  560  	/** Index of first entry to keep */
  561  	firstKeptEntryIndex: number;
  562  	/** Index of user message that starts the turn being split, or -1 if not splitting */
  563  	turnStartIndex: number;
  564  	/** Whether this cut splits a turn (cut point is not a user message) */
  565  	isSplitTurn: boolean;
  566  }
  567
  568  /**
  569   * Find the cut point in session entries that keeps approximately `keepRecentTokens`.
  570   *
  571   * Algorithm: Walk backwards from newest, accumulating estimated message sizes.
  572   * Stop when we've accumulated >= keepRecentTokens. Cut at that point.
  573   *
  574   * Can cut at user OR assistant messages (never tool results). When cutting at an
  575   * assistant message with tool calls, its tool results come after and will be kept.
  576   *
  577   * Returns CutPointResult with:
  578   * - firstKeptEntryIndex: the entry index to start keeping from
  579   * - turnStartIndex: if cutting mid-turn, the user message that started that turn
  580   * - isSplitTurn: whether we're cutting in the middle of a turn
  581   *
  582   * Only considers entries between `startIndex` and `endIndex` (exclusive).
  583   */
  584  export function findCutPoint(
  585  	entries: SessionEntry[],
  586  	startIndex: number,
  587  	endIndex: number,
  588  	keepRecentTokens: number,
  589  ): CutPointResult {
  590  	const cutPoints = findValidCutPoints(entries, startIndex, endIndex);
  591
  592  	if (cutPoints.length === 0) {
  593  		return { firstKeptEntryIndex: startIndex, turnStartIndex: -1, isSplitTurn: false };
  594  	}
  595
  596  	// Walk backwards from newest, accumulating estimated message sizes
  597  	let accumulatedTokens = 0;
  598  	let cutIndex = cutPoints[0]; // Default: keep from first message (not header)
  599
  600  	for (let i = endIndex - 1; i >= startIndex; i--) {
  601  		const entry = entries[i];
  602  		if (entry.type !== "message") continue;
  603
  604  		// Estimate this message's size
  605  		const messageTokens = estimateTokens(entry.message);
  606  		accumulatedTokens += messageTokens;
  607
  608  		// Check if we've exceeded the budget
  609  		if (accumulatedTokens >= keepRecentTokens) {
  610  			// Find the closest valid cut point at or after this entry
  611  			for (let c = 0; c < cutPoints.length; c++) {
  612  				if (cutPoints[c] >= i) {
  613  					cutIndex = cutPoints[c];
  614  					break;
  615  				}
  616  			}
  617  			break;
  618  		}
  619  	}
  620
  621  	// Scan backwards from cutIndex to include any non-message entries (bash, settings, etc.)
  622  	while (cutIndex > startIndex) {
  623  		const prevEntry = entries[cutIndex - 1];
  624  		// Stop at session header or compaction boundaries
  625  		if (prevEntry.type === "compaction") {
  626  			break;
  627  		}
  628  		if (prevEntry.type === "message") {
  629  			// Stop if we hit any message
  630  			break;
  631  		}
  632  		// Include this non-message entry (bash, settings change, etc.)
  633  		cutIndex--;
  634  	}
  635
  636  	// Determine if this is a split turn
  637  	const cutEntry = entries[cutIndex];
  638  	const isUserMessage = cutEntry.type === "message" && cutEntry.message.role === "user";
  639  	const turnStartIndex = isUserMessage ? -1 : findTurnStartIndex(entries, cutIndex, startIndex);
  640
  641  	return {
  642  		firstKeptEntryIndex: cutIndex,
  643  		turnStartIndex,
  644  		isSplitTurn: !isUserMessage && turnStartIndex !== -1,
  645  	};
  646  }
  647

  649  // Summarization
  650  // ============================================================================

  653

  662  function formatAdditionalContext(context: string[] | undefined): string {
  663  	if (!context || context.length === 0) return "";
  664  	const lines = context.map(line => `- ${line}`).join("\n");
  665  	return `<additional-context>\n${lines}\n</additional-context>\n\n`;
  666  }
  667
  668  /**
  669   * Maps the non-special `ThinkingLevel` values to their `Effort` counterparts.
  670   * Exhaustive over the union; throws for `Off`/`Inherit` to surface logic
  671   * errors in callers that forgot to filter those out. Never use a TS cast for
  672   * this — `ThinkingLevel` is a string-union over distinct concepts (Off /
  673   * Inherit are not Efforts), and a cast hides the contract.
  674   */
  675  function effortFromThinkingLevel(level: ThinkingLevel): Effort {
  676  	switch (level) {
  677  		case ThinkingLevel.Minimal:
  678  			return Effort.Minimal;
  679  		case ThinkingLevel.Low:
  680  			return Effort.Low;
  681  		case ThinkingLevel.Medium:
  682  			return Effort.Medium;
  683  		case ThinkingLevel.High:
  684  			return Effort.High;
  685  		case ThinkingLevel.XHigh:
  686  			return Effort.XHigh;
  687  		case ThinkingLevel.Max:
  688  			return Effort.Max;
  689  		case ThinkingLevel.Off:
  690  		case ThinkingLevel.Inherit:
  691  			throw new Error(`effortFromThinkingLevel: ${level} must be handled by caller`);
  692  	}
  693  }
  694
  695  /**
  696   * Resolves the reasoning effort to send on a compaction LLM call.
  697   *
  698   * - Explicit `Off` → `undefined` (omit reasoning entirely; the user said no thinking).
  699   * - `undefined` / `Inherit` → historical `Effort.High` default → clamped per model
  700   *   (preserves current behavior for users who never touched the dial).
  701   * - Explicit effort → respect user choice → clamped per model.
  702   *
  703   * The clamp routes through `clampThinkingLevelForModel`, which returns
  704   * `undefined` for reasoning models without a thinking config — the build-time
  705   * encoding of `compat.supportsReasoningEffort: false` (e.g.
  706   * `xai-oauth/grok-build`). That `undefined` then flows through to the
  707   * openai-responses mapper, which omits the wire param — no

  522  function estimateEntriesTokens(entries: SessionEntry[], startIndex: number, endIndex: number): number {
  523  	let total = 0;
  524  	for (let i = startIndex; i < endIndex; i++) {
  525  		const msg = getMessageFromEntry(entries[i]);
  526  		if (msg) {
  527  			total += estimateTokens(msg);
  528  		}
  529  	}
  530  	return total;

  532
  533  /**
  534   * Find valid cut points: indices of user, assistant, custom, or bashExecution messages.
  535   * Never cut at tool results (they must follow their tool call).
  536   * When we cut at an assistant message with tool calls, its tool results follow it
  537   * and will be kept.
  538   * BashExecutionMessage is treated like a user message (user-initiated context).
  539   */
  540  function findValidCutPoints(entries: SessionEntry[], startIndex: number, endIndex: number): number[] {
  541  	const cutPoints: number[] = [];
  542  	for (let i = startIndex; i < endIndex; i++) {
  543  		const entry = entries[i];
  544  		switch (entry.type) {
  545  			case "message": {
  546  				const role = entry.message.role as string;
  547  				switch (role) {
  548  					case "bashExecution":
  549  					case "hookMessage":
  550  					case "branchSummary":
  551  					case "compactionSummary":
  552  					case "user":
  553  					case "assistant":
  554  						cutPoints.push(i);
  555  						break;
  556  					case "toolResult":
  557  						break;
  558  				}
  559  				break;
  560  			}
  561  			case "thinking_level_change":
  562  			case "model_change":
  563  			case "compaction":
  564  			case "branch_summary":
  565  			case "custom":
  566  			case "custom_message":
  567  			case "label":
  568  		}
  569  		// branch_summary and custom_message are user-role messages, valid cut points
  570  		if (entry.type === "branch_summary" || entry.type === "custom_message") {
  571  			cutPoints.push(i);
  572  		}
  573  	}
  574  	return cutPoints;
  575  }
  576
  577  /**
  578   * Find the user message (or bashExecution) that starts the turn containing the given entry index.
  579   * Returns -1 if no turn start found before the index.
  580   * BashExecutionMessage is treated like a user message for turn boundaries.
  581   */
  582  export function findTurnStartIndex(entries: SessionEntry[], entryIndex: number, startIndex: number): number {
  583  	for (let i = entryIndex; i >= startIndex; i--) {
  584  		const entry = entries[i];
  585  		// branch_summary and custom_message are user-role messages, can start a turn
  586  		if (entry.type === "branch_summary" || entry.type === "custom_message") {
  587  			return i;
  588  		}
  589  		if (entry.type === "message") {
  590  			const role = entry.message.role as string;
  591  			if (role === "user" || role === "bashExecution") {
  592  				return i;
  593  			}
  594  		}
  595  	}
  596  	return -1;
  597  }
  598
  599  export interface CutPointResult {
  600  	/** Index of first entry to keep */
  601  	firstKeptEntryIndex: number;
  602  	/** Index of user message that starts the turn being split, or -1 if not splitting */
  603  	turnStartIndex: number;
  604  	/** Whether this cut splits a turn (cut point is not a user message) */
  605  	isSplitTurn: boolean;
  606  }
  607
  608  /**
  609   * Find the cut point in session entries that keeps approximately `keepRecentTokens`.
  610   *
  611   * Algorithm: Walk backwards from newest, accumulating estimated message sizes.
  612   * Stop when we've accumulated >= keepRecentTokens. Cut at that point.
  613   *
  614   * Can cut at user OR assistant messages (never tool results). When cutting at an
  615   * assistant message with tool calls, its tool results come after and will be kept.
  616   *
  617   * Returns CutPointResult with:
  618   * - firstKeptEntryIndex: the entry index to start keeping from
  619   * - turnStartIndex: if cutting mid-turn, the user message that started that turn
  620   * - isSplitTurn: whether we're cutting in the middle of a turn
  621   *
  622   * Only considers entries between `startIndex` and `endIndex` (exclusive).
  623   */
  624  export function findCutPoint(
  625  	entries: SessionEntry[],
  626  	startIndex: number,
  627  	endIndex: number,
  628  	keepRecentTokens: number,
  629  ): CutPointResult {
  630  	const cutPoints = findValidCutPoints(entries, startIndex, endIndex);
  631
  632  	if (cutPoints.length === 0) {
  633  		return { firstKeptEntryIndex: startIndex, turnStartIndex: -1, isSplitTurn: false };
  634  	}
  635
  636  	// Walk backwards from newest, accumulating estimated message sizes
  637  	let accumulatedTokens = 0;
  638  	let cutIndex = cutPoints[0]; // Default: keep from first message (not header)
  639
  640  	for (let i = endIndex - 1; i >= startIndex; i--) {
  641  		const entry = entries[i];
  642  		if (entry.type !== "message") continue;
  643
  644  		// Estimate this message's size
  645  		const messageTokens = estimateTokens(entry.message);
  646  		accumulatedTokens += messageTokens;
  647
  648  		// Check if we've exceeded the budget
  649  		if (accumulatedTokens >= keepRecentTokens) {
  650  			// Find the closest valid cut point at or after this entry
  651  			for (let c = 0; c < cutPoints.length; c++) {
  652  				if (cutPoints[c] >= i) {
  653  					cutIndex = cutPoints[c];
  654  					break;
  655  				}
  656  			}
  657  			break;
  658  		}
  659  	}
  660
  661  	// Scan backwards from cutIndex to include any non-message entries (bash, settings, etc.)
  662  	while (cutIndex > startIndex) {
  663  		const prevEntry = entries[cutIndex - 1];
  664  		// Stop at session header or compaction boundaries
  665  		if (prevEntry.type === "compaction") {
  666  			break;
  667  		}
  668  		if (prevEntry.type === "message") {
  669  			// Stop if we hit any message
  670  			break;
  671  		}
  672  		// Include this non-message entry (bash, settings change, etc.)
  673  		cutIndex--;
  674  	}
  675
  676  	// Determine if this is a split turn
  677  	const cutEntry = entries[cutIndex];
  678  	const isUserMessage = cutEntry.type === "message" && cutEntry.message.role === "user";
  679  	const turnStartIndex = isUserMessage ? -1 : findTurnStartIndex(entries, cutIndex, startIndex);
  680
  681  	return {
  682  		firstKeptEntryIndex: cutIndex,
  683  		turnStartIndex,
  684  		isSplitTurn: !isUserMessage && turnStartIndex !== -1,
  685  	};
  686  }
  687
  688  // ============================================================================
  689  // Summarization
  690  // ============================================================================
  691
  692  const SUMMARIZATION_PROMPT = prompt.render(compactionSummaryPrompt);
  693
  694  const UPDATE_SUMMARIZATION_PROMPT = prompt.render(compactionUpdateSummaryPrompt);
  695
  696  const SHORT_SUMMARY_PROMPT = prompt.render(compactionShortSummaryPrompt);
  697
  698  const HANDOFF_DOCUMENT_PROMPT = prompt.render(handoffDocumentPrompt);
  699
  700  export const AUTO_HANDOFF_THRESHOLD_FOCUS = prompt.render(autoHandoffThresholdFocusPrompt);
  701
  702  function formatAdditionalContext(context: string[] | undefined): string {
  703  	if (!context || context.length === 0) return "";
  704  	const lines = context.map(line => `- ${line}`).join("\n");
  705  	return `<additional-context>\n${lines}\n</additional-context>\n\n`;
  706  }

06
L1factmodelContext

06 · 工具输出还有独立 prune/protection/shake 层

源码事实

pruning 可清除已消费和被更新结果，tool-protection 保护关键工具/最近预算；shake 依据角色、工具类型、错误/无用标志和 token 权重删除低价值块，并主动失效消息 token cache。

白话解释

不是等整本对话太厚才总结，旧日志、重复读取和明确无用结果会先做局部瘦身；关键结果和最近工作有保护圈。

对自研 Harness 的含义

上下文治理覆盖工具层，但启发式误删需用回归样本监控。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 1–129
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 130–260
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

   11  	collectToolCallsById,

   18  export interface PruneConfig {
   19  	/** Keep the most recent tool output tokens intact. */
   20  	protectTokens: number;
   21  	/** Only prune if total savings meets this threshold. */
   22  	minimumSavings: number;
   23  	/** Tool-result protection matchers. String entries protect every result from that tool; predicates may inspect the paired tool call. */
   24  	protectedTools: ProtectedToolMatcher[];
   25  	/**
   26  	 * Optional supersede key function (see {@link SupersedePruneConfig.supersedeKey}).
   27  	 * When provided, superseded tool results are pruned first — even inside the
   28  	 * `protectTokens` window — before age-based victims. Absent, behavior is
   29  	 * unchanged.
   30  	 */
   31  	supersedeKey?: SupersedeKeyFn;
   32  	/** Useless-flagged results bypass the protect window (see {@link USELESS_NOTICE}). Default true. */
   33  	pruneUseless?: boolean;
   34  	/**
   35  	 * Compaction boundary: the `firstKeptEntryId` of the latest compaction on
   36  	 * the branch. Entries at indices BEFORE this id are summarized away and never
   37  	 * sent to the model, so mutating them only churns persisted history without
   38  	 * shrinking the prompt — they are skipped. Undefined = no compaction (the
   39  	 * whole branch is sent).
   40  	 */
   41  	keepBoundaryId?: string;
   42  	/**
   43  	 * Prompt-cache guard. When set, a tool result whose all-message suffix
   44  	 * (tokens of every message after it) EXCEEDS this is part of the warm,
   45  	 * already-sent cache prefix: mutating it forces the provider to re-write the
   46  	 * whole suffix (cacheWrite premium). Such results — including superseded and
   47  	 * useless ones, which otherwise bypass {@link protectTokens} — are left for
   48  	 * compaction/shake (which rebuild the cache anyway) to reclaim. Undefined =
   49  	 * no cache guard (legacy: superseded/useless prune at any depth).
   50  	 */
   51  	cacheWarmSuffixTokens?: number;
   52  }

   56  	minimumSavings: 20_000,
   57  	protectedTools: ["skill", isSkillReadToolResult],
   58  	pruneUseless: true,

   76   * supersedes selector-carrying reads of the same base path). Return

  101  	 * Undefined = no compaction (the whole branch is sent).

  105  	protectedTools: ProtectedToolMatcher[];

  108  const DEFAULT_SUFFIX_TOKEN_LIMIT = 8_000;

  111  function createPrunedNotice(tokens: number): string {
  112  	return `[Output truncated - ${tokens} tokens]`;
  113  }
  114
  115  /**
  116   * Generic age-based pruning floor. Below this, blanking a result to
  117   * `[Output truncated - N tokens]` recovers nothing — the placeholder itself
  118   * costs ~8 tokens, so a sub-floor result grows the context (and churns the
  119   * prompt cache) instead of shrinking it. Superseded/useless results keep their
  120   * own rules: useless already drops no-savings candidates, superseded prunes for
  121   * correctness regardless of size.
  122   */
  123  const MIN_PRUNE_TOKENS = 50;
  124
  125  function getToolResultMessage(entry: SessionEntry): ToolResultMessage | undefined {
  126  	if (entry.type !== "message") return undefined;
  127  	const message = entry.message as AgentMessage;
  128  	if (message.role !== "toolResult") return undefined;
  129  	return message as ToolResultMessage;
  130  }
  131
  132  function estimatePrunedSavings(tokens: number, notice: string): number {
  133  	const noticeTokens = Math.ceil(notice.length / 4);
  134  	return Math.max(0, tokens - noticeTokens);
  135  }
  136
  137  /**
  138   * For each entry index, the estimated token total of all *message* entries
  139   * strictly after it — how much prompt-cache content the provider must re-write
  140   * (cacheWrite premium) if that entry is mutated in place. Used to keep prune
  141   * mutations inside the cheap-to-recache tail.
  142   */
  143  function computeMessageSuffixTokens(entries: readonly SessionEntry[]): number[] {
  144  	const suffix = new Array<number>(entries.length);
  145  	let accumulated = 0;
  146  	for (let i = entries.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
  147  		suffix[i] = accumulated;
  148  		const entry = entries[i];
  149  		if (entry.type === "message") accumulated += estimateTokens(entry.message as AgentMessage);
  150  	}
  151  	return suffix;
  152  }
  153
  154  /**
  155   * Resolve the array index of the compaction boundary (`keepBoundaryId`). Entries
  156   * before this index are summarized away by the latest compaction and never sent,
  157   * so prune passes must not mutate them. Returns 0 when there is no boundary (no
  158   * compaction → whole branch is sent) or the id is absent from `entries`.
  159   */
  160  function resolveBoundaryIndex(entries: readonly SessionEntry[], keepBoundaryId: string | undefined): number {
  161  	if (keepBoundaryId === undefined) return 0;
  162  	const index = entries.findIndex(entry => entry.id === keepBoundaryId);
  163  	return index < 0 ? 0 : index;
  164  }
  165
  166  interface SupersedeCandidate {
  167  	entry: SessionMessageEntry;
  168  	message: ToolResultMessage;
  169  	/** Index of the entry within the `entries` array. */
  170  	index: number;
  171  	tokens: number;
  172  	/** Placeholder text written over the blanked result. */
  173  	notice: string;
  174  }
  175
  176  /**
  177   * Collect superseded tool results: for every unpruned, unprotected tool result
  178   * whose paired call resolves a supersede key, a LATER result with the same key
  179   * — or with a key that is the `"\u0000"`-prefix parent of this one — marks it
  180   * superseded. Returned in message order.
  181   */
  182  function collectSupersededResults(
  183  	entries: readonly SessionEntry[],
  184  	toolCallsById: ReadonlyMap<string, AgentToolCall>,
  185  	supersedeKey: SupersedeKeyFn,
  186  	protectedTools: readonly ProtectedToolMatcher[],
  187  ): SupersedeCandidate[] {
  188  	const candidates: SupersedeCandidate[] = [];
  189  	const seenKeys = new Set<string>();
  190  	for (let i = entries.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
  191  		const entry = entries[i];
  192  		const message = getToolResultMessage(entry);
  193  		if (!message || message.prunedAt !== undefined) continue;
  194  		const toolCall = toolCallsById.get(message.toolCallId);
  195  		if (!toolCall) continue;
  196  		if (isProtectedToolResult(message, toolCall, protectedTools)) continue;
  197  		const key = supersedeKey(toolCall.name, toolCall.arguments as Record<string, unknown>);
  198  		if (key === undefined) continue;
  199  		const separator = key.indexOf("\u0000");
  200  		const superseded = seenKeys.has(key) || (separator >= 0 && seenKeys.has(key.slice(0, separator)));
  201  		seenKeys.add(key);
  202  		if (!superseded) continue;
  203  		candidates.push({
  204  			entry: entry as SessionMessageEntry,
  205  			message,
  206  			index: i,
  207  			tokens: estimateTokens(message as AgentMessage),
  208  			notice: SUPERSEDED_NOTICE,
  209  		});
  210  	}
  211  	return candidates.reverse();
  212  }
  213
  214  /**
  215   * Collect tool results their tool flagged contextually useless (zero matches,
  216   * elapsed wait): unpruned, non-error, unprotected, not in `exclude`, and large
  217   * enough that blanking to {@link USELESS_NOTICE} actually saves tokens.
  218   * Returned in message order.
  219   */
  220  function collectUselessResults(
  221  	entries: readonly SessionEntry[],
  222  	toolCallsById: ReadonlyMap<string, AgentToolCall>,
  223  	protectedTools: readonly ProtectedToolMatcher[],
  224  	exclude: ReadonlySet<ToolResultMessage>,
  225  ): SupersedeCandidate[] {
  226  	const candidates: SupersedeCandidate[] = [];
  227  	for (let i = 0; i < entries.length; i++) {
  228  		const entry = entries[i];
  229  		const message = getToolResultMessage(entry);
  230  		if (message?.useless !== true || message.prunedAt !== undefined || message.isError === true) continue;
  231  		if (exclude.has(message)) continue;
  232  		if (isProtectedToolResult(message, toolCallsById.get(message.toolCallId), protectedTools)) continue;
  233  		const tokens = estimateTokens(message as AgentMessage);
  234  		if (estimatePrunedSavings(tokens, USELESS_NOTICE) <= 0) continue;
  235  		candidates.push({ entry: entry as SessionMessageEntry, message, index: i, tokens, notice: USELESS_NOTICE });
  236  	}
  237  	return candidates;
  238  }
  239
  240  /**
  241   * Prune superseded tool results (e.g. stale `read` outputs replaced by a newer
  242   * read of the same file) and, when `pruneUseless` is set, results their tool
  243   * flagged contextually useless. Cheap, incremental, and prompt-cache-aware: a
  244   * candidate is pruned now only when the suffix after it is small (tail case —
  245   * the read→edit→read loop) or when the context has been idle long enough that
  246   * the provider cache is cold anyway (then all still-sent candidates flush).
  247   * Never mutates entries before `keepBoundaryId` (summarized away — not sent).
  248   */
  249  export function pruneSupersededToolResults(entries: SessionEntry[], config: SupersedePruneConfig): PruneResult {
  250  	const toolCallsById = collectToolCallsById(entries);
  251  	const candidates = config.supersedeKey
  252  		? collectSupersededResults(entries, toolCallsById, config.supersedeKey, config.protectedTools)
  253  		: [];
  254  	if (config.pruneUseless) {
  255  		const exclude = new Set(candidates.map(candidate => candidate.message));
  256  		candidates.push(...collectUselessResults(entries, toolCallsById, config.protectedTools, exclude));
  257  		candidates.sort((a, b) => a.index - b.index);
  258  	}
  259  	if (candidates.length === 0) return { prunedCount: 0, tokensSaved: 0 };
  260
  261  	const now = config.now ?? Date.now();
  262  	let lastMessageTimestamp: number | undefined;
  263  	for (let i = entries.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
  264  		const entry = entries[i];
  265  		if (entry.type !== "message") continue;
  266  		const timestamp = (entry.message as AgentMessage).timestamp;
  267  		if (typeof timestamp === "number") lastMessageTimestamp = timestamp;
  268  		break;
  269  	}
  270  	const idle =
  271  		lastMessageTimestamp !== undefined && now - lastMessageTimestamp >= (config.idleFlushMs ?? DEFAULT_IDLE_FLUSH_MS);
  272
  273  	const boundaryIndex = resolveBoundaryIndex(entries, config.keepBoundaryId);
  274
  275  	let toPrune: SupersedeCandidate[];
  276  	if (idle) {
  277  		// Provider cache is cold (idle exceeds the retention TTL), so re-writing
  278  		// the sent region costs nothing. Entries before the compaction boundary
  279  		// are summarized away and never sent — skip them to avoid pointless churn.
  280  		toPrune = candidates.filter(candidate => candidate.index >= boundaryIndex);
  281  	} else {
  282  		const suffixTokenLimit = config.suffixTokenLimit ?? DEFAULT_SUFFIX_TOKEN_LIMIT;
  283  		// suffixTokens[i] = estimated tokens of all messages strictly after entry i.
  284  		// Mutating a candidate re-writes its suffix in the warm cache, so prune only
  285  		// when that suffix is small (cheap-to-recache tail) and the candidate sits
  286  		// at/after the compaction boundary.
  287  		const suffixTokens = computeMessageSuffixTokens(entries);
  288  		toPrune = candidates.filter(
  289  			candidate => candidate.index >= boundaryIndex && suffixTokens[candidate.index] <= suffixTokenLimit,
  290  		);
  291  	}
  292  	if (toPrune.length === 0) return { prunedCount: 0, tokensSaved: 0 };
  293
  294  	const prunedAt = Date.now();
  295  	let tokensSaved = 0;
  296  	for (const candidate of toPrune) {
  297  		candidate.message.content = [{ type: "text", text: candidate.notice }];
  298  		candidate.message.prunedAt = prunedAt;
  299  		invalidateMessageCache(candidate.message as AgentMessage);
  300  		tokensSaved += estimatePrunedSavings(candidate.tokens, candidate.notice);
  301  	}
  302  	return { prunedCount: toPrune.length, tokensSaved };
  303  }
  304
  305  export function pruneToolOutputs(entries: SessionEntry[], config: PruneConfig = DEFAULT_PRUNE_CONFIG): PruneResult {
  306  	let accumulatedTokens = 0;
  307  	let tokensSaved = 0;
  308  	let prunedCount = 0;
  309
  310  	const candidates: Array<{ entry: SessionMessageEntry; tokens: number; superseded: boolean; useless: boolean }> = [];
  311  	const toolCallsById = collectToolCallsById(entries);
  312  	const supersededMessages = config.supersedeKey
  313  		? new Set(
  314  				collectSupersededResults(entries, toolCallsById, config.supersedeKey, config.protectedTools).map(
  315  					candidate => candidate.message,
  316  				),
  317  			)
  318  		: undefined;
  319  	const uselessMessages =
  320  		config.pruneUseless !== false
  321  			? new Set(
  322  					collectUselessResults(
  323  						entries,
  324  						toolCallsById,
  325  						config.protectedTools,
  326  						supersededMessages ?? new Set(),
  327  					).map(candidate => candidate.message),
  328  				)
  329  			: undefined;
  330
  331  	const boundaryIndex = resolveBoundaryIndex(entries, config.keepBoundaryId);

07
L1factmodelContext

07 · 长期记忆有 off/local/Mnemopi/Hindsight 四种后端

源码事实

runtime 以 memory.backend 为唯一选择器；local 将 rollout 摘要写 SQLite/markdown 且 learned.md 可写但不可结构化搜索，Mnemopi 是本地 SQLite+embedding+实体/三元组/时间/召回/整合管线，Hindsight 为远端后端。主 session 才启动并动态刷新 memory tools/system prompt。

白话解释

记忆可以关掉、只做本地日志总结、启用本地语义记忆库，或接远端记忆服务；子 Agent 默认不各自建长期脑库。

对自研 Harness 的含义

上下文和长期记忆分层清楚，但多后端的隐私、成本与一致性需要治理。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 6–14
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 15–24
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

    7   * Pick the active memory backend for a Settings instance.

   10   * through this):
   11   *   - `memory.backend === "hindsight"`  → Hindsight remote memory
   12   *   - `memory.backend === "mnemopi"`  → local Mnemopi SQLite memory

   14   *   - everything else                   → no-op
   15   *
   16   * `memories.enabled` remains accepted only as a legacy migration input. Once
   17   * a config is loaded, `memory.backend` is the sole runtime selector.

   21  	if (id === "hindsight") return (await import("../hindsight/backend")).hindsightBackend;
   22  	if (id === "mnemopi") return (await import("../mnemopi/backend")).mnemopiBackend;
   23  	if (id === "local") return localBackend;
   24  	return offBackend;
   25  }

L1facttools

01 · 内建工具面远超文件与 shell

源码事实

registry 包含 read/bash/edit/write、AST grep/edit、LSP、eval kernels、glob/grep、browser、computer、GitHub、web search、image inspect/gen、checkpoint/rewind、todo/goal、task/hub/yield、memory tools、skills 管理等；SDK 再合并 extension/custom/MCP/RPC host tools。

02
L1risktools

02 · Approval 分级完整，但默认是 yolo

源码事实

工具声明 read/write/exec tier 和可按参数计算的 policy；user per-tool allow/prompt/deny 高优先级。always-ask 只自动 read，write 自动 read+write，yolo 自动所有 tier；settings 默认 tools.approvalMode=yolo。

白话解释

门卫机制很成熟，但默认把门敞开；用户不改设置时，bash、browser、task 等执行级工具通常不询问。

对自研 Harness 的含义

面向个人效率是合理选择，企业默认策略应反转为 write 或 always-ask。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 13–25
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 26–39
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。
