   13  export type ApprovalPolicy = "allow" | "deny" | "prompt";
   14  export type ApprovalMode = "always-ask" | "write" | "yolo";
   15
   16  type ApprovalSubject = Pick<AgentTool, "name" | "approval" | "formatApprovalDetails">;
   17
   18  export interface ResolvedApproval {
   19  	policy: ApprovalPolicy;
   20  	tier: ToolTier;
   21  	reason?: string;
   22  	override: boolean;
   23  	source?: "tool" | "user" | "mode";
   24  }
   25
   26  const POLICY_VALUES: ReadonlySet<ApprovalPolicy> = new Set(["allow", "deny", "prompt"]);
   27  const TIER_VALUES: ReadonlySet<ToolTier> = new Set(["read", "write", "exec"]);
   28
   29  const TIER_RANK: Record<ToolTier, number> = {
   30  	read: 0,
   31  	write: 1,
   32  	exec: 2,
   33  };
   34
   35  const APPROVAL_MODE_MAX_TIER: Record<ApprovalMode, ToolTier> = {
   36  	"always-ask": "read",
   37  	write: "write",
   38  	yolo: "exec",
   39  };
   40
   41  const DEFAULT_PROMPT_TRUNCATE_CHARS = 2000;
   42
   43  /** Best-effort conversion of an arbitrary user-supplied value to a policy. */
   44  function normalizePolicy(value: unknown): ApprovalPolicy | undefined {
   45  	if (typeof value !== "string") return undefined;
   46  	const lowered = value.trim().toLowerCase();
   47  	return POLICY_VALUES.has(lowered as ApprovalPolicy) ? (lowered as ApprovalPolicy) : undefined;
   48  }
   49
   50  function isToolTier(value: unknown): value is ToolTier {
   51  	return typeof value === "string" && TIER_VALUES.has(value as ToolTier);
   52  }
   53
   54  function normalizeDecision(value: unknown): Omit<ResolvedApproval, "policy"> & { policy?: ApprovalPolicy } {
   55  	if (isToolTier(value)) {
   56  		return { tier: value, override: false };
   57  	}
   58
   59  	if (value && typeof value === "object" && !Array.isArray(value)) {
   60  		const record = value as Record<string, unknown>;
   61  		const tier = isToolTier(record.tier) ? record.tier : "exec";
   62  		const reason = typeof record.reason === "string" && record.reason.length > 0 ? record.reason : undefined;
   63  		const policy = normalizePolicy(record.policy);
   64  		return {
   65  			tier,
   66  			override: record.override === true,
   67  			...(policy ? { policy } : {}),
   68  			...(reason ? { reason } : {}),
   69  		};
   70  	}
   71
   72  	return { tier: "exec", override: false };
   73  }
   74
   75  function getToolDecision(
   76  	tool: ApprovalSubject,
   77  	args: unknown,
   78  ): Omit<ResolvedApproval, "policy"> & { policy?: ApprovalPolicy } {
   79  	const approval = tool.approval;
   80  	const decision: ToolApprovalDecision | undefined = typeof approval === "function" ? approval(args) : approval;
   81  	return normalizeDecision(decision);
   82  }
   83
   84  /**
   85   * Evaluate a tool's own approval declaration against `args` and return the
   86   * resulting capability tier, defaulting to `exec` when the tool omits an
   87   * approval. Unlike reading `tool.approval` directly, this runs function-valued
   88   * approvals — the write tool's `xd://` gate uses it to take a mounted device's
   89   * argument-dependent tier instead of falling back to `exec`.
   90   */
   91  export function resolveToolTier(tool: ApprovalSubject, args: unknown): ToolTier {
   92  	return getToolDecision(tool, args).tier;
   93  }
   94
   95  function modeApprovesTier(mode: ApprovalMode, tier: ToolTier): boolean {
   96  	return TIER_RANK[tier] <= TIER_RANK[APPROVAL_MODE_MAX_TIER[mode]];
   97  }
   98
   99  /**
  100   * Resolve approval policy for a tool call.
  101   *
  102   * Resolution order:
  103   *  1. Tool `approval(args)` decision, defaulting to tier "exec" when omitted.
  104   *  2. User per-tool override, if set and valid.
  105   *  3. Active mode tier comparison.
  106   *
  107   * In yolo mode, override-based tool prompts are ignored; user `tools.approval`
  108   * settings remain authoritative.
  109   */
  110  export function resolveApproval(

   13  export type ApprovalPolicy = "allow" | "deny" | "prompt";

   18  export interface ResolvedApproval {
   19  	policy: ApprovalPolicy;
   20  	tier: ToolTier;
   21  	reason?: string;
   22  	override: boolean;
   23  	source?: "tool" | "user" | "mode";
   24  	/** User-policy key that produced `source: "user"` (defaults to the tool name). */
   25  	policyKey?: string;
   26  }
   27
   28  const POLICY_VALUES: ReadonlySet<ApprovalPolicy> = new Set(["allow", "deny", "prompt"]);
   29  const TIER_VALUES: ReadonlySet<ToolTier> = new Set(["read", "write", "exec"]);
   30
   31  const TIER_RANK: Record<ToolTier, number> = {
   32  	read: 0,
   33  	write: 1,
   34  	exec: 2,
   35  };
   36
   37  const APPROVAL_MODE_MAX_TIER: Record<ApprovalMode, ToolTier> = {
   38  	"always-ask": "read",
   39  	write: "write",
   40  	yolo: "exec",
   41  };
   42
   43  const DEFAULT_PROMPT_TRUNCATE_CHARS = 2000;
   44
   45  /** Best-effort conversion of an arbitrary user-supplied value to a policy. */
   46  function normalizePolicy(value: unknown): ApprovalPolicy | undefined {
   47  	if (typeof value !== "string") return undefined;
   48  	const lowered = value.trim().toLowerCase();
   49  	return POLICY_VALUES.has(lowered as ApprovalPolicy) ? (lowered as ApprovalPolicy) : undefined;
   50  }
   51
   52  function isToolTier(value: unknown): value is ToolTier {
   53  	return typeof value === "string" && TIER_VALUES.has(value as ToolTier);
   54  }
   55
   56  function normalizeDecision(value: unknown): Omit<ResolvedApproval, "policy"> & { policy?: ApprovalPolicy } {
   57  	if (isToolTier(value)) {
   58  		return { tier: value, override: false };
   59  	}
   60
   61  	if (value && typeof value === "object" && !Array.isArray(value)) {
   62  		const record = value as Record<string, unknown>;
   63  		const tier = isToolTier(record.tier) ? record.tier : "exec";
   64  		const reason = typeof record.reason === "string" && record.reason.length > 0 ? record.reason : undefined;
   65  		const policy = normalizePolicy(record.policy);
   66  		const policyKey =
   67  			typeof record.policyKey === "string" && record.policyKey.length > 0 ? record.policyKey : undefined;
   68  		return {
   69  			tier,
   70  			override: record.override === true,
   71  			...(policy ? { policy } : {}),
   72  			...(reason ? { reason } : {}),
   73  			...(policyKey ? { policyKey } : {}),
   74  		};
   75  	}
   76
   77  	return { tier: "exec", override: false };
   78  }

   93   * approvals — the write tool's `xd://` gate uses it to take a mounted device's
   94   * argument-dependent tier instead of falling back to `exec`.
   95   */

   97  	return getToolDecision(tool, args).tier;
   98  }

  101  	return TIER_RANK[tier] <= TIER_RANK[APPROVAL_MODE_MAX_TIER[mode]];

  105   * Resolve approval policy for a tool call.
  106   *
  107   * Resolution order:

03
L1limitationtools

03 · 主 Agent 默认宿主执行；子任务 isolation 默认 none 且主要隔离工作区

源码事实

bash runner 使用宿主 shell/native process；源码未给主 Agent 建容器/namespace sandbox。task isolation 可选 APFS/btrfs/ZFS/reflink/overlayfs/ProjFS/block clone/worktree/copy，并用 patch 或 branch 合并，但默认 mode=none。

白话解释

子工人可以拿一份独立项目副本，防止同时改乱代码；这不自动隔离网络、进程和主机秘密，而且默认连副本也不开。

对自研 Harness 的含义

工作区隔离与安全沙箱必须分开标注；无人值守仍需外层 VM/container。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 1–109
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 110–220
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

   20  	sessionId: string;
   21  	refs: number;
   22  	destination?: BashAppendDestination;
   23  	pending?: Promise<BashAppendDestination>;
   24  }
   25
   26  interface PendingBashMessage {
   27  	target: BashSessionTarget;
   28  	message: BashExecutionMessage;
   29  }
   30
   31  /** Ownership snapshot spanning a session or branch transition. */
   32  export interface BashSessionTransition {
   33  	oldTarget: BashSessionTarget;
   34  	newTarget: BashSessionTarget;
   35  	oldSessionId: string;
   36  	oldSessionFile: string | undefined;
   37  	oldLeafId: string | null;
   38  	detachedManager: SessionManager | undefined;
   39  	resolveOld: ((destination: BashAppendDestination) => void) | undefined;
   40  	resolveNew: (destination: BashAppendDestination) => void;
   41  }
   42
   43  /** Capabilities the bash runner borrows from its owning session. */
   44  export interface BashRunnerHost {
   45  	agent: Agent;
   46  	sessionManager: SessionManager;
   47  	settings: Settings;
   48  	extensionRunner(): ExtensionRunner | undefined;
   49  	isStreaming(): boolean;
   50  }
   51
   52  /** Owns bash execution and preserves result ownership across transcript transitions. */
   53  export class BashRunner {
   54  	readonly #host: BashRunnerHost;
   55  	#abortControllers = new Set<AbortController>();
   56  	#pendingMessages: PendingBashMessage[] = [];
   57  	#sessionTarget: BashSessionTarget;
   58
   59  	constructor(host: BashRunnerHost) {
   60  		this.#host = host;
   61  		this.#sessionTarget = {
   62  			sessionId: host.sessionManager.getSessionId(),
   63  			refs: 0,
   64  			destination: { kind: "current", manager: host.sessionManager },
   65  		};
   66  	}
   67
   68  	/** Executes a bash command while retaining the session and branch that owned its start. */
   69  	async executeBash(
   70  		command: string,
   71  		onChunk?: (chunk: string) => void,
   72  		options?: { excludeFromContext?: boolean; useUserShell?: boolean },
   73  	): Promise<BashResult> {
   74  		const target = this.#captureSessionTarget();
   75  		let targetTransferred = false;
   76  		const excludeFromContext = options?.excludeFromContext === true;
   77  		const cwd = this.#host.sessionManager.getCwd();
   78  		try {
   79  			const extensionRunner = this.#host.extensionRunner();
   80  			if (extensionRunner?.hasHandlers("user_bash")) {
   81  				const hookResult = await extensionRunner.emitUserBash({
   82  					type: "user_bash",
   83  					command,
   84  					excludeFromContext,
   85  					cwd,
   86  				});
   87  				if (hookResult?.result) {
   88  					targetTransferred = true;
   89  					await this.#recordResultForTarget(target, command, hookResult.result, options);
   90  					return hookResult.result;
   91  				}
   92  			}
   93
   94  			const abortController = new AbortController();
   95  			this.#abortControllers.add(abortController);
   96  			let result: BashResult;
   97  			try {
   98  				result = await executeBashCommand(command, {
   99  					onChunk,
  100  					signal: abortController.signal,
  101  					sessionKey: target.sessionId,
  102  					cwd,
  103  					timeout: clampTimeout("bash", undefined, this.#host.settings.get("tools.maxTimeout")) * 1000,
  104  					onMinimizedSave: originalText => this.#saveOriginalArtifact(target, originalText),
  105  					useUserShell: options?.useUserShell,
  106  				});
  107  			} finally {
  108  				this.#abortControllers.delete(abortController);
  109  			}
  110  			targetTransferred = true;
  111  			await this.#recordResultForTarget(target, command, result, options);
  112  			return result;
  113  		} finally {
  114  			if (!targetTransferred) await this.#releaseSessionTarget(target);
  115  		}
  116  	}
  117
  118  	/** Records a bash result supplied outside executeBash in the current ownership scope. */
  119  	recordBashResult(command: string, result: BashResult, options?: { excludeFromContext?: boolean }): void {
  120  		const target = this.#captureSessionTarget();
  121  		const message = this.#createMessage(command, result, options);
  122  		if (this.#host.isStreaming() && target === this.#sessionTarget) {
  123  			this.#pendingMessages.push({ target, message });
  124  			return;
  125  		}
  126  		if (target.destination) {
  127  			try {
  128  				this.#appendMessage(target.destination, message);
  129  			} finally {
  130  				void this.#releaseSessionTarget(target);
  131  			}
  132  			return;
  133  		}
  134  		void this.#appendOwnedMessage(target, message).catch(error => {
  135  			logger.error("Failed to record bash result in its owning session", { error: String(error) });
  136  		});
  137  	}
  138
  139  	/** Cancels every running bash command. */
  140  	abort(): void {
  141  		for (const abortController of this.#abortControllers) abortController.abort();
  142  	}
  143
  144  	/** Whether a bash command is currently running. */
  145  	get isRunning(): boolean {
  146  		return this.#abortControllers.size > 0;
  147  	}
  148
  149  	/** Whether bash results are waiting for a safe persistence boundary. */
  150  	get hasPendingMessages(): boolean {
  151  		return this.#pendingMessages.length > 0;
  152  	}
  153
  154  	/** Flushes deferred bash results without changing their captured ownership. */
  155  	async flushPending(): Promise<void> {
  156  		if (this.#pendingMessages.length === 0) return;
  157  		const pending = this.#pendingMessages;
  158  		this.#pendingMessages = [];
  159  		for (const { target, message } of pending) await this.#appendOwnedMessage(target, message);
  160  	}
  161
  162  	/** Runs a leaf rewrite while retaining in-flight bash on its originating branch. */
  163  	withBranchTransition<T>(mutate: () => T): T {
  164  		const transition = this.beginSessionTransition();
  165  		let transitioned = false;
  166  		try {
  167  			const result = mutate();
  168  			this.markSessionTransition(transition);
  169  			transitioned = true;
  170  			return result;
  171  		} finally {
  172  			this.finishSessionTransition(transition, transitioned);
  173  		}
  174  	}
  175
  176  	/** Snapshots the owner of in-flight bash before a session or branch transition. */
  177  	beginSessionTransition(options?: { persistDetached?: boolean }): BashSessionTransition {
  178  		const oldTarget = this.#sessionTarget;
  179  		let detachedManager: SessionManager | undefined;
  180  		let resolveOld: ((destination: BashAppendDestination) => void) | undefined;
  181  		if (oldTarget.refs > 0) {
  182  			detachedManager = this.#host.sessionManager.cloneCurrentSession({ persist: options?.persistDetached });
  183  			const pendingOld = Promise.withResolvers<BashAppendDestination>();
  184  			oldTarget.destination = undefined;
  185  			oldTarget.pending = pendingOld.promise;
  186  			resolveOld = pendingOld.resolve;
  187  		}
  188  		const pendingNew = Promise.withResolvers<BashAppendDestination>();
  189  		return {
  190  			oldTarget,
  191  			newTarget: {
  192  				sessionId: this.#host.sessionManager.getSessionId(),
  193  				refs: 0,
  194  				pending: pendingNew.promise,
  195  			},
  196  			oldSessionId: this.#host.sessionManager.getSessionId(),
  197  			oldSessionFile: this.#host.sessionManager.getSessionFile(),
  198  			oldLeafId: this.#host.sessionManager.getLeafId(),
  199  			detachedManager,
  200  			resolveOld,
  201  			resolveNew: pendingNew.resolve,
  202  		};
  203  	}
  204
  205  	/** Adopts a transition's new target as the live bash owner. */
  206  	markSessionTransition(transition: BashSessionTransition): void {
  207  		transition.newTarget.sessionId = this.#host.sessionManager.getSessionId();
  208  		this.#sessionTarget = transition.newTarget;
  209  	}
  210
  211  	/** Resolves destinations opened by beginSessionTransition. */
  212  	finishSessionTransition(transition: BashSessionTransition, success: boolean): void {
  213  		const manager = this.#host.sessionManager;
  214  		const currentDestination: BashAppendDestination = { kind: "current", manager };
  215  		let oldDestination: BashAppendDestination = currentDestination;
  216  		if (success && transition.resolveOld) {
  217  			const currentFile = manager.getSessionFile();
  218  			const sameFile =
  219  				transition.oldSessionFile === currentFile ||
  220  				(transition.oldSessionFile !== undefined &&
  221  					currentFile !== undefined &&
  222  					path.resolve(transition.oldSessionFile) === path.resolve(currentFile));
  223  			const sameSession = transition.oldSessionId === manager.getSessionId() && sameFile;
  224  			if (sameSession) {
  225  				oldDestination =
  226  					transition.oldLeafId === manager.getLeafId()
  227  						? currentDestination
  228  						: { kind: "branch", manager, parentId: transition.oldLeafId };
  229  			} else if (transition.detachedManager) {
  230  				oldDestination = { kind: "detached", manager: transition.detachedManager };
  231  			}
  232  		}
  233  		if (transition.resolveOld) {
  234  			transition.oldTarget.pending = undefined;
  235  			transition.oldTarget.destination = oldDestination;
  236  			transition.resolveOld(oldDestination);
  237  		}
  238  		transition.newTarget.pending = undefined;
  239  		transition.newTarget.destination = currentDestination;
  240  		if (!success) transition.newTarget.sessionId = manager.getSessionId();
  241  		transition.resolveNew(currentDestination);
  242  		if (transition.detachedManager && (oldDestination.kind !== "detached" || transition.oldTarget.refs === 0)) {
  243  			void transition.detachedManager.close().catch(error => {
  244  				logger.warn("Failed to close detached bash session writer", { error: String(error) });
  245  			});
  246  		}
  247  	}
  248
  249  	async #saveOriginalArtifact(target: BashSessionTarget, originalText: string): Promise<string | undefined> {
  250  		try {
  251  			const destination = target.destination ?? (await target.pending);
  252  			return await destination?.manager.saveArtifact(originalText, "bash-original");
  253  		} catch {
  254  			return undefined;
  255  		}
  256  	}
  257
  258  	#createMessage(
  259  		command: string,
  260  		result: BashResult,
  261  		options?: { excludeFromContext?: boolean },
  262  	): BashExecutionMessage {
  263  		const meta = outputMeta().truncationFromSummary(result, { direction: "tail" }).get();
  264  		return {
  265  			role: "bashExecution",
  266  			command,
  267  			output: result.output,
  268  			exitCode: result.exitCode,
  269  			cancelled: result.cancelled,
  270  			truncated: result.truncated,
  271  			meta,
  272  			timestamp: Date.now(),
  273  			excludeFromContext: options?.excludeFromContext,
  274  		};
  275  	}
  276
  277  	#captureSessionTarget(): BashSessionTarget {
  278  		this.#sessionTarget.refs++;
  279  		return this.#sessionTarget;
  280  	}
  281
  282  	async #releaseSessionTarget(target: BashSessionTarget): Promise<void> {
  283  		if (target.refs <= 0) throw new Error("Bash session target released more than once");
  284  		target.refs--;
  285  		if (target.refs === 0 && target.destination?.kind === "detached") await target.destination.manager.close();
  286  	}
  287
  288  	#appendMessage(destination: BashAppendDestination, message: BashExecutionMessage): void {
  289  		switch (destination.kind) {
  290  			case "current":
  291  				this.#host.agent.appendMessage(message);

06
DIMENSION · CONNECTORS

连接器、插件与 MCP

2 条可定位结论；每条按事实 → 白话 → 工程影响 → 源码摘录展开。

01
L1factconnectors

01 · MCP 是完整内建连接器，不是插件样例

源码事实

MCPManager 从多来源配置发现服务器，并行连接、缓存 tools、订阅 resources、接收 notifications、动态刷新；transport 支持 stdio、legacy SSE、Streamable HTTP，HTTP 保存 session id、处理 server request/notification、401/403 刷新后重试。

白话解释

它不仅能调用 MCP 工具，还管理连接、认证、资源订阅、服务端推送和重连生命周期。

对自研 Harness 的含义

连接器成熟度高，适合企业工具生态；同时需审计远端 URL、headers 和本地 command 配置。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 282–330
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 331–380
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

  326  	 * Discover and connect to all MCP servers from .mcp.json files.
  327  	 * Returns tools and any connection errors.

  329  	async discoverAndConnect(options?: MCPDiscoverOptions): Promise<MCPLoadResult> {
  330  		let loadedConfigs: LoadMCPConfigsResult;
  331  		try {
  332  			loadedConfigs = await loadAllMCPConfigs(this.cwd, {
  333  				enableProjectConfig: options?.enableProjectConfig,
  334  				filterExa: options?.filterExa,
  335  				filterBrowser: options?.filterBrowser,
  336  			});
  337  		} catch (error) {

  339  			options?.onStatus?.({ type: "failed", serverName: ".mcp.json", error: message });
  340  			throw error;

  348  	/**
  349  	 * Connect to specific MCP servers.
  350  	 * Connections are made in parallel for faster startup.
  351  	 */
  352  	async connectServers(
  353  		configs: Record<string, MCPServerConfig>,
  354  		sources: Record<string, SourceMeta>,
  355  		onStatus?: (event: McpConnectionStatusEvent) => void,
  356  	): Promise<MCPLoadResult> {
  357  		type ConnectionTask = {
  358  			name: string;
  359  			config: MCPServerConfig;
  360  			tracked: TrackedPromise<ToolLoadResult>;
  361  			toolsPromise: Promise<ToolLoadResult>;
  362  		};
  363
  364  		const errors = new Map<string, string>();
  365  		const connectedServers = new Set<string>();
  366  		const allTools: CustomTool<TSchema, MCPToolDetails>[] = [];
  367  		const reportedErrors = new Set<string>();
  368  		let allowBackgroundLogging = false;
  369  		const statusServerNames: string[] = [];
  370  		const validationFailures: Array<{ name: string; message: string }> = [];
  371
  372  		// Prepare connection tasks
  373  		const connectionTasks: ConnectionTask[] = [];
  374
  375  		for (const [name, config] of Object.entries(configs)) {
  376  			if (sources[name]) {
  377  				this.#sources.set(name, sources[name]);
  378  				const existing = this.#connections.get(name);
  379  				if (existing) {
  380  					existing._source = sources[name];
  381  				}
  382  			}
  383
  384  			// Skip if already connected
  385  			if (this.#connections.has(name)) {
  386  				connectedServers.add(name);
  387  				continue;
  388  			}
  389
  390  			if (
  391  				this.#pendingConnections.has(name) ||
  392  				this.#pendingToolLoads.has(name) ||
  393  				this.#pendingReconnections.has(name)
  394  			) {
  395  				continue;
  396  			}
  397
  398  			statusServerNames.push(name);
  399
  400  			// Validate config
  401  			const validationErrors = validateServerConfig(name, config);
  402  			if (validationErrors.length > 0) {
  403  				const message = validationErrors.join("; ");
  404  				errors.set(name, message);
  405  				validationFailures.push({ name, message });
  406  				reportedErrors.add(name);
  407  				continue;
  408  			}
  409
  410  			// Save config early so reconnection works even if the initial connect times out
  411  			// and falls back to cached/deferred tools.
  412  			this.#serverConfigs.set(name, config);
  413
  414  			// Resolve auth config before connecting, but do so per-server in parallel.
  415  			const connectionPromise = (async () => {
  416  				const resolvedConfig = await this.#resolveAuthConfig(config);
  417  				return connectToServer(name, resolvedConfig, {
  418  					onNotification: (method, params) => {
  419  						this.#handleServerNotification(name, method, params);
  420  					},
  421  					onRequest: (method, params) => {
  422  						return this.#handleServerRequest(method, params);
  423  					},
  424  				});
  425  			})().then(
  426  				connection => {
  427  					// Store original config (without resolved tokens) to keep
  428  					// cache keys stable and avoid leaking rotating credentials.
  429  					connection.config = config;
  430  					this.#serverConfigs.set(name, config);
  431  					if (sources[name]) {
  432  						connection._source = sources[name];
  433  					}
  434  					if (this.#pendingConnections.get(name) === connectionPromise) {
  435  						this.#pendingConnections.delete(name);
  436  						this.#connections.set(name, connection);
  437  					}
  438
  439  					// Wire auth refresh for HTTP-like transports so 401s trigger token refresh.
  440  					// Gate on a resolvable managed credential, not on the auth block:
  441  					// definition-only configs (url-keyed fallback) get Bearer injection
  442  					// too and need the same mid-session refresh hook.
  443  					if (
  444  						isAuthRefreshableMCPTransport(connection.transport) &&
  445  						lookupMcpOAuthCredential(this.#authStorage, config)
  446  					) {
  447  						connection.transport.onAuthError = async () => {
  448  							const refreshed = await this.#resolveAuthConfig(config, { forceRefresh: true });
  449  							if (refreshed.type === "http" || refreshed.type === "sse") {
  450  								return refreshed.headers ?? null;
  451  							}

  292  	 * Set a callback to fire when any server's tools change.
  293  	 *
  294  	 * May return a Promise; if so, {@link refreshServerTools} awaits it so that
  295  	 * downstream consumers (e.g. `mcp_notification` listeners for
  296  	 * `notifications/tools/list_changed`) observe not just the manager's
  297  	 * refreshed tool set but also any session-level rebind driven by the
  298  	 * handler (`session.refreshMCPTools`). Other callsites (initial connect,
  299  	 * disconnect, reconnect) invoke the handler synchronously — their downstream
  300  	 * chains don't need to serialize on the rebind.

  317  		this.#onPromptsChanged = handler;
  318  		// Fire immediately for servers that already have prompts loaded
  319  		for (const [name, connection] of this.#connections) {
  320  			if (connection.prompts?.length) {
  321  				handler(name);
  322  			}
  323  		}
  324  	}
  325
  326  	#subscribeAndTrack(name: string, connection: MCPServerConnection, uris: string[], notificationEpoch: number): void {
  327  		void subscribeToResources(connection, uris)
  328  			.then(() => {
  329  				const action = resolveSubscriptionPostAction(
  330  					this.#notificationsEnabled,
  331  					this.#notificationsEpoch,
  332  					notificationEpoch,
  333  				);
  334  				if (action === "rollback") {
  335  					void unsubscribeFromResources(connection, uris).catch(error => {
  336  						logger.debug("Failed to rollback stale MCP resource subscription", {
  337  							path: `mcp:${name}`,
  338  							error,
  339  						});
  340  					});
  341  					return;
  342  				}
  343  				if (action === "ignore") {
  344  					return;
  345  				}
  346  				this.#subscribedResources.set(name, new Set(uris));
  347  			})
  348  			.catch(error => {
  349  				logger.debug("Failed to subscribe to MCP resources", { path: `mcp:${name}`, error });
  350  			});
  351  	}
  352
  353  	setNotificationsEnabled(enabled: boolean): void {
  354  		const wasEnabled = this.#notificationsEnabled;
  355  		this.#notificationsEnabled = enabled;
  356  		if (enabled === wasEnabled) return;
  357
  358  		this.#notificationsEpoch += 1;
  359  		const notificationEpoch = this.#notificationsEpoch;
  360
  361  		if (enabled) {
  362  			// Subscribe to all connected servers that support it
  363  			for (const [name, connection] of this.#connections) {
  364  				if (connection.capabilities.resources?.subscribe && connection.resources) {
  365  					const uris = connection.resources.map(r => r.uri);
  366  					this.#subscribeAndTrack(name, connection, uris, notificationEpoch);
  367  				}
  368  			}
  369  			return;
  370  		}
  371
  372  		// Unsubscribe from all servers
  373  		for (const [name, connection] of this.#connections) {
  374  			const uris = this.#subscribedResources.get(name);
  375  			if (uris && uris.size > 0) {
  376  				void unsubscribeFromResources(connection, Array.from(uris)).catch(error => {
  377  					logger.debug("Failed to unsubscribe MCP resources", { path: `mcp:${name}`, error });
  378  				});
  379  			}
  380  		}
  381  		this.#subscribedResources.clear();
  382  	}

02
L1factconnectors

02 · 可直接吸收 Claude、Gemini、OpenCode 等生态的 MCP 配置

源码事实

capability discovery providers 解析本产品、Claude、Gemini CLI、OpenCode、Codex、Cline、Cursor、VSCode/Windsurf 等配置；OpenCode local/remote 和 Gemini mcpServers 被归一成同一 stdio/SSE/HTTP contract，并保留 source metadata。

白话解释

用户换工具时不必手工重写全部 MCP 配置，OMP 会读取其他 Agent 的配置并统一格式。

对自研 Harness 的含义

迁移体验突出，但跨生态自动加载需有来源可见性和项目 trust。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 88–126
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 127–167
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

   91  /** OpenCode MCP server config (from opencode.json "mcp" key) */
   92  interface OpenCodeMCPConfig {
   93  	type?: "local" | "remote";
   94  	command?: string | string[];
   95  	args?: string[];
   96  	env?: Record<string, string>;
   97  	environment?: Record<string, string>;
   98  	url?: string;
   99  	headers?: Record<string, string>;
  100  	enabled?: boolean;
  101  	timeout?: number;
  102  }

  104  function stringArray(value: unknown): string[] | undefined {
  105  	if (!Array.isArray(value)) return undefined;
  106  	for (const item of value) {
  107  		if (typeof item !== "string") return undefined;
  108  	}
  109  	return value;
  110  }
  111
  112  function stringRecord(value: unknown): Record<string, string> | undefined {
  113  	if (!value || typeof value !== "object" || Array.isArray(value)) return undefined;
  114
  115  	const record: Record<string, string> = {};
  116  	for (const [key, item] of Object.entries(value)) {
  117  		if (typeof item !== "string") return undefined;
  118  		record[key] = item;
  119  	}
  120  	return record;
  121  }
  122
  123  function normalizeCommand(
  124  	commandValue: string | string[] | undefined,
  125  	argsValue: unknown,
  126  ): { command: string | undefined; args: string[] | undefined } {
  127  	const configuredArgs = stringArray(argsValue);
  128  	if (Array.isArray(commandValue)) {
  129  		const [command, ...commandArgs] = commandValue;
  130  		const args = configuredArgs ? [...commandArgs, ...configuredArgs] : commandArgs;
  131  		return {
  132  			command: typeof command === "string" ? command : undefined,
  133  			args: args.length > 0 ? args : undefined,
  134  		};
  135  	}
  136
  137  	return {
  138  		command: typeof commandValue === "string" ? commandValue : undefined,
  139  		args: configuredArgs && configuredArgs.length > 0 ? configuredArgs : undefined,
  140  	};
  141  }
  142
  143  async function loadMCPServers(ctx: LoadContext): Promise<LoadResult<MCPServer>> {
  144  	const items: MCPServer[] = [];
  145  	const warnings: string[] = [];
  146
  147  	// User-level: ~/.config/opencode/opencode.json
  148  	const userConfigPath = getUserPath(ctx, "opencode", "opencode.json");
  149  	if (userConfigPath) {
  150  		const config = await loadJsonConfig(userConfigPath);
  151  		if (config) {
  152  			const result = extractMCPServers(config, userConfigPath, "user");
  153  			items.push(...result.items);
  154  			if (result.warnings) warnings.push(...result.warnings);
  155  		}
  156  	}
  157
  158  	// Project-level: opencode.json in project root
  159  	const projectConfigPath = path.join(ctx.cwd, "opencode.json");
  160  	const projectConfig = await loadJsonConfig(projectConfigPath);
  161  	if (projectConfig) {
  162  		const result = extractMCPServers(projectConfig, projectConfigPath, "project");
  163  		items.push(...result.items);
  164  		if (result.warnings) warnings.push(...result.warnings);
  165  	}
  166
  167  	return { items, warnings };
  168  }
  169
  170  function extractMCPServers(
  171  	config: Record<string, unknown>,
  172  	configPath: string,
  173  	level: "user" | "project",
  174  ): LoadResult<MCPServer> {
  175  	const items: MCPServer[] = [];
  176  	const warnings: string[] = [];
  177
  178  	if (!config.mcp || typeof config.mcp !== "object") {
  179  		return { items, warnings };
  180  	}
  181
  182  	const servers = expandEnvVarsDeep(config.mcp as Record<string, unknown>);
  183
  184  	for (const [name, raw] of Object.entries(servers)) {
  185  		if (!raw || typeof raw !== "object") {
  186  			warnings.push(`Invalid MCP config for "${name}" in ${configPath}`);
  187  			continue;
  188  		}
  189
  190  		const serverConfig = raw as OpenCodeMCPConfig;
  191
  192  		// Determine transport from OpenCode's "type" field
  193  		let transport: "stdio" | "sse" | "http" | undefined;
  194  		if (serverConfig.type === "local") {
  195  			transport = "stdio";
  196  		} else if (serverConfig.type === "remote") {
  197  			transport = "http";
  198  		} else if (serverConfig.url) {
  199  			transport = "http";
  200  		} else if (serverConfig.command) {
  201  			transport = "stdio";
  202  		}
  203
  204  		const command = normalizeCommand(serverConfig.command, serverConfig.args);
  205  		const env = stringRecord(serverConfig.environment) ?? stringRecord(serverConfig.env);
  206
  207  		items.push({
  208  			name,
  209  			command: command.command,
  210  			args: command.args,
  211  			env,
  212  			url: typeof serverConfig.url === "string" ? serverConfig.url : undefined,
  213  			headers: serverConfig.headers && typeof serverConfig.headers === "object" ? serverConfig.headers : undefined,
  214  			enabled: serverConfig.enabled,
  215  			timeout: typeof serverConfig.timeout === "number" ? serverConfig.timeout : undefined,
  216  			transport,
  217  			_source: createSourceMeta(PROVIDER_ID, configPath, level),
  218  		});
  219  	}
  220
  221  	return { items, warnings };
  222  }

  238  				providerId: PROVIDER_ID,

   90  function getConfigSources(ctx: LoadContext): OpenCodeConfigSource[] {
   91  	const sources: OpenCodeConfigSource[] = [];
   92  	for (const filename of CONFIG_FILENAMES) {
   93  		const configPath = getUserPath(ctx, "opencode", filename);
   94  		if (configPath) sources.push({ path: configPath, level: "user" });
   95  	}
   96  	for (const filename of CONFIG_FILENAMES) {
   97  		sources.push({ path: path.join(ctx.cwd, filename), level: "project" });
   98  	}
   99  	for (const filename of CONFIG_FILENAMES) {
  100  		const configPath = getProjectPath(ctx, "opencode", filename);
  101  		if (configPath) sources.push({ path: configPath, level: "project" });
  102  	}
  103  	return sources;

  184  	};
  185  }
  186
  187  async function loadMCPServers(ctx: LoadContext): Promise<LoadResult<MCPServer>> {
  188  	const warnings: string[] = [];
  189
  190  	// Deep-merge each server across config layers in ascending precedence, the
  191  	// way OpenCode itself merges configs, so a partial higher-precedence override
  192  	// (e.g. project opencode.jsonc setting only mcp.<name>.timeout) inherits the
  193  	// command/url from lower-precedence layers instead of shadowing the complete
  194  	// definition and being rejected by mcpCapability.validate.
  195  	const mergedByName = new Map<string, Record<string, unknown>>();
  196  	const sourceByName = new Map<string, OpenCodeConfigSource>();
  197
  198  	for (const source of getConfigSources(ctx)) {
  199  		const config = await loadJsonConfig(source.path, configPath => {
  200  			logger.warn("Failed to parse OpenCode config", { path: configPath });
  201  		});
  202  		if (!config || !isRecord(config.mcp)) continue;
  203
  204  		for (const name in config.mcp) {
  205  			const raw = config.mcp[name];
  206  			if (!isRecord(raw)) {
  207  				warnings.push(`Invalid MCP config for "${name}" in ${source.path}`);
  208  				continue;
  209  			}
  210  			const previous = mergedByName.get(name);
  211  			mergedByName.set(name, previous ? mergeConfigRecords(previous, raw) : raw);
  212  			sourceByName.set(name, source);
  213  		}
  214  	}
  215
  216  	const items: MCPServer[] = [];
  217  	for (const [name, config] of mergedByName) {
  218  		const serverConfig = expandEnvVarsDeep(config) as OpenCodeMCPConfig;
  219  		const source = sourceByName.get(name)!;
  220  		items.push(buildMCPServer(name, serverConfig, source));
  221  	}
  222
  223  	return { items, warnings };
  224  }
  225
  226  /** Deep-merge two OpenCode config records; `override` wins, nested records recurse. */
  227  function mergeConfigRecords(base: Record<string, unknown>, override: Record<string, unknown>): Record<string, unknown> {
  228  	const result: Record<string, unknown> = { ...base };
  229  	for (const key in override) {
  230  		const value = override[key];
  231  		const existing = result[key];
  232  		result[key] = isRecord(existing) && isRecord(value) ? mergeConfigRecords(existing, value) : value;
  233  	}
  234  	return result;
  235  }
  236
  237  /** Translate one merged OpenCode MCP entry into the canonical MCPServer shape. */
  238  function buildMCPServer(name: string, serverConfig: OpenCodeMCPConfig, source: OpenCodeConfigSource): MCPServer {
08
DIMENSION · OBSERVABILITY

观测、日志与回放

2 条可定位结论；每条按事实 → 白话 → 工程影响 → 源码摘录展开。

01
L1factobservability

01 · 会话是树形事件账本，存储层可替换

源码事实

SessionManager 保留 parentId 树、message/compaction/branch/model/thinking/custom/session_init 等 entry；storage contract 支持 append/read/list/lock，仓库实现 indexed local、SQL、Redis 等后端并把大对象放 blob/artifact store。

白话解释

聊天、分支、压缩和配置变化都作为事件保存；存哪里可以从个人本地换到服务端数据库。

对自研 Harness 的含义

同一 Harness 可从 CLI 扩到多用户服务，但多后端一致性/锁/迁移成为核心基础设施。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 1–129
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 130–260
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

   19  	 *

   32   * Optional guard applied by {@link SessionStorage.writeTextAtomic}. The
   33   * backend MUST call `commitGuard()` synchronously immediately before it makes
   34   * the staged content visible at `path`. If it returns `false`, the staged
   35   * write is discarded and the target is left untouched. Backends MUST NOT
   36   * yield between calling the guard and publishing the write, so a concurrent
   37   * synchronous rewrite that took over cannot be overwritten by a stale body.

   72  	 * {@link IndexedSessionStorage}) await their per-path queues so a caller
   73  	 * driving a graceful shutdown does not exit while a fire-and-forget
   74  	 * `writeTextSync` publish is still on the wire.

   79  // FinalizationRegistry to clean up leaked file descriptors
   80  const writerRegistry = new FinalizationRegistry<number>(fd => {
   81  	try {
   82  		fs.closeSync(fd);
   83  	} catch {
   84  		// Ignore - fd may already be closed or invalid
   85  	}
   86  });
   87
   88  class FileSessionStorageWriter implements SessionStorageWriter {
   89  	#fd: number;
   90  	#closed = false;
   91  	#error: Error | undefined;
   92  	#onError: ((err: Error) => void) | undefined;
   93
   94  	constructor(fpath: string, options?: { flags?: "a" | "w"; onError?: (err: Error) => void }) {
   95  		this.#onError = options?.onError;
   96  		const flags = options?.flags ?? "a";
   97  		// Ensure parent directory exists
   98  		const dir = path.dirname(fpath);
   99  		if (!fs.existsSync(dir)) {
  100  			fs.mkdirSync(dir, { recursive: true });
  101  		}
  102  		// Open file once, keep fd for lifetime
  103  		this.#fd = fs.openSync(fpath, flags === "w" ? "w" : "a");
  104  		// Register for cleanup if abandoned without close()
  105  		writerRegistry.register(this, this.#fd, this);
  106  	}
  107
  108  	#recordError(err: unknown): Error {
  109  		const error = toError(err);
  110  		if (!this.#error) this.#error = error;
  111  		this.#onError?.(error);
  112  		return error;
  113  	}
  114
  115  	async append(line: string): Promise<void> {
  116  		if (this.#closed) throw new Error("Writer closed");
  117  		if (this.#error) throw this.#error;
  118  		try {
  119  			const buf = Buffer.from(line, "utf-8");
  120  			let offset = 0;
  121  			while (offset < buf.length) {
  122  				const written = fs.writeSync(this.#fd, buf, offset, buf.length - offset);
  123  				if (written === 0) {
  124  					throw new Error("Short write");
  125  				}
  126  				offset += written;
  127  			}
  128  		} catch (err) {
  129  			throw this.#recordError(err);
  130  		}
  131  	}
  132
  133  	async flush(): Promise<void> {
  134  		if (this.#error) throw this.#error;
  135  	}
  136
  137  	isOpen(): boolean {
  138  		return !this.#closed;
  139  	}
  140
  141  	async close(): Promise<void> {
  142  		if (this.#closed) return;
  143  		this.#closed = true;
  144  		// Unregister from finalization - we're closing properly
  145  		writerRegistry.unregister(this);
  146  		try {
  147  			fs.closeSync(this.#fd);
  148  		} catch {
  149  			// Ignore close errors
  150  		}
  151  	}
  152
  153  	getError(): Error | undefined {
  154  		return this.#error;
  155  	}
  156  }
  157
  158  export class FileSessionStorage implements SessionStorage {
  159  	ensureDirSync(dir: string): void {
  160  		if (!fs.existsSync(dir)) {
  161  			fs.mkdirSync(dir, { recursive: true });
  162  		}
  163  	}
  164
  165  	existsSync(path: string): boolean {
  166  		return fs.existsSync(path);
  167  	}
  168
  169  	writeTextSync(fpath: string, content: string): void {
  170  		const dir = path.dirname(fpath);
  171  		this.ensureDirSync(dir);
  172  		const tempPath = path.join(dir, `.${path.basename(fpath)}.${Snowflake.next()}.tmp`);
  173  		try {
  174  			fs.writeFileSync(tempPath, content);
  175  			fs.renameSync(tempPath, fpath);
  176  		} catch (err) {
  177  			try {
  178  				if (fs.existsSync(tempPath)) fs.unlinkSync(tempPath);
  179  			} catch (cleanupErr) {
  180  				if (!isEnoent(cleanupErr)) {
  181  					logger.warn("Failed to remove session rewrite temp file", {
  182  						sessionFile: fpath,
  183  						tempPath,
  184  						error: toError(cleanupErr).message,
  185  					});
  186  				}
  187  			}
  188  			if (hasFsCode(err, "EPERM")) {
  189  				fs.writeFileSync(fpath, content);
  190  				return;
  191  			}
  192  			throw toError(err);
  193  		}
  194  	}
  195
  196  	async updateSessionTitle(fpath: string, update: SessionTitleUpdate): Promise<void> {
  197  		const fd = fs.openSync(fpath, "r+");
  198  		try {
  199  			const buf = Buffer.from(serializeTitleSlot(update), "utf-8");
  200  			let offset = 0;
  201  			while (offset < buf.length) {
  202  				const written = fs.writeSync(fd, buf, offset, buf.length - offset, offset);
  203  				if (written === 0) {
  204  					throw new Error("Short write");
  205  				}
  206  				offset += written;
  207  			}
  208  		} catch (err) {
  209  			throw toError(err);
  210  		} finally {
  211  			fs.closeSync(fd);
  212  		}
  213  	}
  214
  215  	statSync(path: string): SessionStorageStat {
  216  		const stats = fs.statSync(path);
  217  		return { size: stats.size, mtimeMs: stats.mtimeMs, mtime: stats.mtime };
  218  	}
  219
  220  	listFilesSync(dir: string, pattern: string): string[] {
  221  		try {
  222  			return Array.from(new Bun.Glob(pattern).scanSync(dir)).map(name => path.join(dir, name));
  223  		} catch {
  224  			return [];
  225  		}
  226  	}
  227
  228  	async exists(path: string): Promise<boolean> {
  229  		try {
  230  			await fs.promises.access(path);
  231  			return true;
  232  		} catch (err) {
  233  			if (isEnoent(err)) return false;
  234  			throw err;
  235  		}
  236  	}
  237
  238  	readText(path: string): Promise<string> {
  239  		return Bun.file(path).text();
  240  	}
  241
  242  	async readTextSlices(path: string, prefixBytes: number, suffixBytes: number): Promise<[string, string]> {
  243  		return peekFileEnds(path, prefixBytes, suffixBytes, (head, tail) => [
  244  			utf8Decoder.decode(head),
  245  			utf8Decoder.decode(tail),
  246  		]);
  247  	}
  248
  249  	async writeText(path: string, content: string): Promise<void> {
  250  		await Bun.write(path, content, { createPath: true });
  251  	}
  252
  253  	async writeTextAtomic(fpath: string, content: string, options?: WriteTextAtomicOptions): Promise<void> {
  254  		const dir = path.resolve(fpath, "..");
  255  		const tempPath = path.join(dir, `.${path.basename(fpath)}.${Snowflake.next()}.tmp`);
  256  		await fs.promises.mkdir(dir, { recursive: true });
  257  		try {
  258  			await fs.promises.writeFile(tempPath, content);
  259  		} catch (err) {
  260  			this.#discardTemp(tempPath, fpath);
  261  			throw toError(err);
  262  		}
  263  		// Guard-check + rename MUST NOT be separated by an await. A concurrent
  264  		// synchronous rewrite (flushSync -> #rewriteSynchronously) can otherwise
  265  		// publish a fresh body between the check and the rename, and this stale
  266  		// staged body would overwrite it. Sync rename closes that window.
  267  		if (options?.commitGuard && !options.commitGuard()) {
  268  			this.#discardTemp(tempPath, fpath);
  269  			return;
  270  		}
  271  		try {
  272  			this.renameSync(tempPath, fpath);
  273  			return;
  274  		} catch (err) {
  275  			if (!hasFsCode(err, "EPERM")) {
  276  				this.#discardTemp(tempPath, fpath);
  277  				throw toError(err);
  278  			}
  279  			try {
  280  				this.#replaceSessionFileAfterEpermSync(tempPath, fpath, err, options?.commitGuard);
  281  			} catch (fallbackErr) {
  282  				this.#discardTemp(tempPath, fpath);
  283  				throw fallbackErr;
  284  			}
  285  		}
  286  	}
  287
  288  	/**
  289  	 * Sync rename hook. Split from `rename` so `writeTextAtomic` can perform its
  290  	 * guard-then-publish step without a yield, and so tests can inject
  291  	 * Windows-style EPERM at the sync layer used by the atomic path.
  292  	 */
  293  	renameSync(source: string, target: string): void {
  294  		fs.renameSync(source, target);
  295  	}
  296
  297  	#discardTemp(tempPath: string, targetPath: string): void {
  298  		try {
  299  			fs.unlinkSync(tempPath);
  300  		} catch (err) {
  301  			if (!isEnoent(err)) {
  302  				logger.warn("Failed to remove session rewrite temp file", {
  303  					sessionFile: targetPath,
  304  					tempPath,
  305  					error: toError(err).message,
  306  				});
  307  			}
  308  		}
  309  	}
  310
  311  	#replaceSessionFileAfterEpermSync(
  312  		tempPath: string,
  313  		targetPath: string,
  314  		renameError: unknown,
  315  		commitGuard?: () => boolean,
  316  	): void {
  317  		const dir = path.resolve(targetPath, "..");
  318  		const backupPath = path.join(dir, `${path.basename(targetPath)}.${Snowflake.next()}.bak`);
  319  		try {
  320  			this.renameSync(targetPath, backupPath);
  321  		} catch (moveAsideError) {
  322  			if (isEnoent(moveAsideError)) {
  323  				if (commitGuard && !commitGuard()) {
  324  					this.#discardTemp(tempPath, targetPath);
  325  					return;
  326  				}
  327  				this.renameSync(tempPath, targetPath);
  328  				return;
  329  			}
  330  			throw toError(renameError);
  331  		}

    9  export interface SessionStorageStat {
   10  	size: number;
   11  	mtimeMs: number;
   12  	mtime: Date;
   13  }
   14
   15  export interface SessionStorageWriter {
   16  	/**
   17  	 * Append one newline-terminated line.
   18  	 *
   19  	 * File and memory storage apply the line synchronously before the returned
   20  	 * promise settles, so a software crash after `append` returns (or after a
   21  	 * fire-and-forget call begins) still sees the entry on disk / in body. No
   22  	 * `fsync` — power loss may still drop the last page. Indexed backends update
   23  	 * the local index immediately and queue the remote publish in call order.
   24  	 *
   25  	 * `line` MUST include the trailing newline.
   26  	 */
   27  	append(line: string): Promise<void>;
   28  	/**
   29  	 * Synchronous append when the backend can apply the line before return.
   30  	 * File and memory implement this so {@link SessionManager} can latch the
   31  	 * first write failure before the appending call returns (surfaced by a later
   32  	 * flushSync/close/next append — the turn loop does not throw from append).
   33  	 * Indexed backends update the local index immediately and queue remote I/O.
   34  	 */
   35  	appendSync?(line: string): void;
   36  	/** Resolve once all queued appends complete. No fsync. */
   37  	flush(): Promise<void>;
   38  	/** Drain synchronously flushable queued work when the backend supports it. No fsync. */
   39  	flushSync?(): void;
   40  	/** False once close() has begun/finished. */
   41  	isOpen(): boolean;
   42  	close(): Promise<void>;
   43  	getError(): Error | undefined;
   44  }
   45
   46  /**
   47   * Optional guard applied by {@link SessionStorage.writeTextAtomic}. The
   48   * backend MUST call `commitGuard()` synchronously immediately before it makes
   49   * the staged content visible at `path`. If it returns `false`, the staged
   50   * write is discarded and the target is left untouched. Backends MUST NOT
   51   * yield between calling the guard and publishing the write, so a concurrent
   52   * synchronous rewrite that took over cannot be overwritten by a stale body.
   53   */
   54  export interface WriteTextAtomicOptions {
   55  	commitGuard?: () => boolean;
   56  }
   57
   58  export interface SessionStorage {
   59  	ensureDirSync(dir: string): void;
   60  	existsSync(path: string): boolean;
   61  	writeTextSync(path: string, content: string): void;
   62  	/**
   63  	 * Update the current session title through the storage backend.
   64  	 *
   65  	 * File-like backends rewrite the fixed-width JSONL title slot; indexed
   66  	 * backends can store the semantic title fields and synthesize the slot when
   67  	 * reading.
   68  	 */
   69  	updateSessionTitle(path: string, update: SessionTitleUpdate): Promise<void>;
   70  	statSync(path: string): SessionStorageStat;
   71  	listFilesSync(dir: string, pattern: string): string[];
   72
   73  	exists(path: string): Promise<boolean>;
   74  	readText(path: string): Promise<string>;
   75  	/** Read the requested UTF-8 byte windows from the head and tail of the file. */
   76  	readTextSlices(path: string, prefixBytes: number, suffixBytes: number): Promise<[string, string]>;
   77  	writeText(path: string, content: string): Promise<void>;
   78  	writeTextAtomic(path: string, content: string, options?: WriteTextAtomicOptions): Promise<void>;
   79  	rename(path: string, nextPath: string): Promise<void>;
   80  	unlink(path: string): Promise<void>;
   81  	deleteSessionWithArtifacts(sessionPath: string): Promise<void>;
   82  	openWriter(path: string, options?: { flags?: "a" | "w"; onError?: (err: Error) => void }): SessionStorageWriter;
   83  	/**
   84  	 * Wait for every backing write scheduled by this storage to become durably
   85  	 * visible. Sync backends (file, memory) return immediately because their
   86  	 * writes complete in-body; async backends (Redis/SQL via
   87  	 * {@link IndexedSessionStorage}) await their per-path queues so a caller
   88  	 * driving a graceful shutdown does not exit while a fire-and-forget
   89  	 * `writeTextSync` publish is still on the wire.
   90  	 */
   91  	drain(): Promise<void>;
   92  }
   93
   94  // FinalizationRegistry to clean up leaked file descriptors
   95  const writerRegistry = new FinalizationRegistry<number>(fd => {
   96  	try {
   97  		fs.closeSync(fd);
   98  	} catch {
   99  		// Ignore - fd may already be closed or invalid
  100  	}
  101  });
  102
  103  class FileSessionStorageWriter implements SessionStorageWriter {
  104  	#fd: number;
  105  	#closed = false;
  106  	#error: Error | undefined;
  107  	#onError: ((err: Error) => void) | undefined;
  108
  109  	constructor(fpath: string, options?: { flags?: "a" | "w"; onError?: (err: Error) => void }) {
  110  		this.#onError = options?.onError;
  111  		const flags = options?.flags ?? "a";
  112  		// Ensure parent directory exists
  113  		const dir = path.dirname(fpath);
  114  		if (!fs.existsSync(dir)) {
  115  			fs.mkdirSync(dir, { recursive: true });
  116  		}
  117  		// Open file once, keep fd for lifetime
  118  		this.#fd = fs.openSync(fpath, flags === "w" ? "w" : "a");
  119  		// Register for cleanup if abandoned without close()
  120  		writerRegistry.register(this, this.#fd, this);
  121  	}
  122
  123  	#recordError(err: unknown): Error {
  124  		const error = toError(err);
  125  		if (!this.#error) this.#error = error;
  126  		this.#onError?.(error);
  127  		return error;
  128  	}
  129
  130  	#writeNow(line: string): void {
  131  		const buf = Buffer.from(line, "utf-8");
  132  		let offset = 0;
  133  		while (offset < buf.length) {
  134  			const written = fs.writeSync(this.#fd, buf, offset, buf.length - offset);
  135  			if (written === 0) {
  136  				throw new Error("Short write");
  137  			}
  138  			offset += written;
  139  		}
  140  	}
  141
  142  	appendSync(line: string): void {
  143  		if (this.#closed) throw new Error("Writer closed");
  144  		if (this.#error) throw this.#error;
  145  		// Write in-body so software crash after the call still sees the entry.
  146  		// Microtask batching used to leave completed transcript lines only in
  147  		// memory until the next event-loop turn; process crash then lost every
  148  		// post-checkpoint event. flush/flushSync remain no-op drains (no fsync).
  149  		try {
  150  			this.#writeNow(line);
  151  		} catch (err) {
  152  			throw this.#recordError(err);
  153  		}
  154  	}
  155
  156  	async append(line: string): Promise<void> {
  157  		this.appendSync(line);
  158  	}
  159
  160  	async flush(): Promise<void> {
  161  		if (this.#error) throw this.#error;
  162  	}
  163
  164  	flushSync(): void {
  165  		if (this.#error) throw this.#error;
  166  	}
  167
  168  	isOpen(): boolean {
  169  		return !this.#closed;
  170  	}
  171
  172  	async close(): Promise<void> {
  173  		if (this.#closed) return;
  174  		this.#closed = true;
  175  		// Unregister from finalization - we're closing properly
  176  		writerRegistry.unregister(this);
  177  		try {
  178  			fs.closeSync(this.#fd);
  179  		} catch {
  180  			// Ignore close errors
  181  		}
  182  		if (this.#error) throw this.#error;
  183  	}
  184
  185  	getError(): Error | undefined {
  186  		return this.#error;
  187  	}
  188  }
  189
  190  export class FileSessionStorage implements SessionStorage {
  191  	ensureDirSync(dir: string): void {
  192  		if (!fs.existsSync(dir)) {
  193  			fs.mkdirSync(dir, { recursive: true });
  194  		}
  195  	}
  196
  197  	existsSync(path: string): boolean {
  198  		return fs.existsSync(path);
  199  	}
  200
  201  	writeTextSync(fpath: string, content: string): void {
  202  		const dir = path.dirname(fpath);
  203  		this.ensureDirSync(dir);
  204  		const tempPath = path.join(dir, `.${path.basename(fpath)}.${Snowflake.next()}.tmp`);
  205  		try {
  206  			fs.writeFileSync(tempPath, content);
  207  			fs.renameSync(tempPath, fpath);
  208  		} catch (err) {
  209  			try {
  210  				if (fs.existsSync(tempPath)) fs.unlinkSync(tempPath);
  211  			} catch (cleanupErr) {
  212  				if (!isEnoent(cleanupErr)) {
  213  					logger.warn("Failed to remove session rewrite temp file", {
  214  						sessionFile: fpath,
  215  						tempPath,
  216  						error: toError(cleanupErr).message,
  217  					});
  218  				}
  219  			}
  220  			if (hasFsCode(err, "EPERM")) {
  221  				fs.writeFileSync(fpath, content);
  222  				return;
  223  			}
  224  			throw toError(err);
  225  		}
  226  	}
  227
  228  	async updateSessionTitle(fpath: string, update: SessionTitleUpdate): Promise<void> {
  229  		const fd = fs.openSync(fpath, "r+");
  230  		try {
  231  			const buf = Buffer.from(serializeTitleSlot(update), "utf-8");
  232  			let offset = 0;
  233  			while (offset < buf.length) {
  234  				const written = fs.writeSync(fd, buf, offset, buf.length - offset, offset);
  235  				if (written === 0) {
  236  					throw new Error("Short write");
  237  				}
  238  				offset += written;
  239  			}
  240  		} catch (err) {
  241  			throw toError(err);
  242  		} finally {
  243  			fs.closeSync(fd);
  244  		}
  245  	}
  246
  247  	statSync(path: string): SessionStorageStat {
  248  		const stats = fs.statSync(path);
  249  		return { size: stats.size, mtimeMs: stats.mtimeMs, mtime: stats.mtime };
  250  	}
  251
  252  	listFilesSync(dir: string, pattern: string): string[] {
  253  		try {
  254  			return Array.from(new Bun.Glob(pattern).scanSync(dir)).map(name => path.join(dir, name));
  255  		} catch {
  256  			return [];
  257  		}
  258  	}
  259
  260  	async exists(path: string): Promise<boolean> {
  261  		try {
  262  			await fs.promises.access(path);
  263  			return true;
  264  		} catch (err) {
  265  			if (isEnoent(err)) return false;
  266  			throw err;
  267  		}
  268  	}
  269
  270  	readText(path: string): Promise<string> {
  271  		return Bun.file(path).text();
  272  	}
  273
  274  	async readTextSlices(path: string, prefixBytes: number, suffixBytes: number): Promise<[string, string]> {
  275  		return peekFileEnds(path, prefixBytes, suffixBytes, (head, tail) => [
  276  			utf8Decoder.decode(head),
  277  			utf8Decoder.decode(tail),
  278  		]);
  279  	}
  280
  281  	async writeText(path: string, content: string): Promise<void> {
  282  		await Bun.write(path, content, { createPath: true });
  283  	}
  284
  285  	async writeTextAtomic(fpath: string, content: string, options?: WriteTextAtomicOptions): Promise<void> {
  286  		const dir = path.resolve(fpath, "..");
  287  		const tempPath = path.join(dir, `.${path.basename(fpath)}.${Snowflake.next()}.tmp`);
  288  		await fs.promises.mkdir(dir, { recursive: true });
  289  		try {
  290  			await fs.promises.writeFile(tempPath, content);
  291  		} catch (err) {
  292  			this.#discardTemp(tempPath, fpath);
  293  			throw toError(err);
  294  		}
  295  		// Guard-check + rename MUST NOT be separated by an await. A concurrent
  296  		// synchronous rewrite (flushSync -> #rewriteSynchronously) can otherwise
  297  		// publish a fresh body between the check and the rename, and this stale
  298  		// staged body would overwrite it. Sync rename closes that window.
  299  		if (options?.commitGuard && !options.commitGuard()) {
  300  			this.#discardTemp(tempPath, fpath);
  301  			return;
  302  		}
  303  		try {
  304  			this.renameSync(tempPath, fpath);
  305  			return;
  306  		} catch (err) {
  307  			if (!hasFsCode(err, "EPERM")) {
  308  				this.#discardTemp(tempPath, fpath);
  309  				throw toError(err);
  310  			}
  311  			try {
  312  				this.#replaceSessionFileAfterEpermSync(tempPath, fpath, err, options?.commitGuard);
  313  			} catch (fallbackErr) {
  314  				this.#discardTemp(tempPath, fpath);
  315  				throw fallbackErr;
  316  			}
  317  		}
  318  	}
  319
  320  	/**
  321  	 * Sync rename hook. Split from `rename` so `writeTextAtomic` can perform its
  322  	 * guard-then-publish step without a yield, and so tests can inject
  323  	 * Windows-style EPERM at the sync layer used by the atomic path.
  324  	 */
  325  	renameSync(source: string, target: string): void {
  326  		fs.renameSync(source, target);
  327  	}
  328
  329  	#discardTemp(tempPath: string, targetPath: string): void {
  330  		try {
  331  			fs.unlinkSync(tempPath);
02
L1factobservability

02 · 观测层原生实现 OTEL GenAI spans、成本与 run coverage

源码事实

loop 生成 invoke_agent→chat/execute_tool 层级和 handoff，覆盖 GenAI semantic conventions、provider/model/usage/cache/reasoning、tool args/results、TTFT、cost、gateway headers、自定义 attributes；内容捕获默认关闭/可 summary/full。

白话解释

不仅有终端日志，每次模型和工具调用都能变成标准 trace，还能关联费用和网关调用 ID；敏感内容是否进 trace 可配置。

对自研 Harness 的含义

是 14 个项目中企业观测设计最完整的一档。

    1  /**
    2   * OpenTelemetry instrumentation for the agent loop.
    3   *
    4   * Implements the OpenTelemetry GenAI semantic conventions
    5   * (https://opentelemetry.io/docs/specs/semconv/gen-ai/) plus `pi.gen_ai.*`
    6   * extension attributes for run summaries, dashboard summaries, and cost hints
    7   * that are useful to downstream observability UIs.
    8   *
    9   * Span hierarchy emitted by the loop:
   10   *
   11   *   invoke_agent {agent.name}         (one per runLoop, gen_ai.operation.name=invoke_agent)
   12   *   ├── chat {model}                  (one per LLM call, gen_ai.operation.name=chat)
   13   *   ├── execute_tool {tool.name}      (one per tool call, gen_ai.operation.name=execute_tool)
   14   *   └── ...
   15   *
   16   * The `handoff` operation is emitted via the public {@link recordHandoff}
   17   * helper for hosts that route work between named agents.
   18   *
   19   * Activation is opt-in: callers pass an {@link AgentTelemetryConfig} on
   20   * `AgentLoopConfig.telemetry`. When unset, every helper short-circuits and
   21   * the loop performs zero tracer lookups. When set but no OTEL SDK is
   22   * registered, `@opentelemetry/api` returns a no-op tracer and all calls are
   23   * cheap pass-throughs.

01
L1factcollaboration

01 · Task 是内建多 Agent 调度器，支持 batch、async 与结构化 yield

源码事实

TaskTool 发现 bundled/user/project agent；batch call 强制 shared context 和非空 tasks，每项可选 agent/effort/isolation/outputSchema；async 交给 job manager，sync 按 semaphore 并发并合并 usage/artifacts。子 Agent 必须通过 yield 交付，可增量分段并做 schema validation。

白话解释

主 Agent 可以一次发一组有共同背景的任务，每个工人独立选角色和输出合同；结果不是随便一段聊天，而是显式交付。

对自研 Harness 的含义

比 subprocess 示例级子 Agent 更接近真正控制平面。

    入口、配置和状态初始化

   56  	return prompt.render(subagentUserPromptTemplate, {
   57  		assignment: assignment.trim(),

   61  function createUsageTotals(): Usage {
   62  	return {
   63  		input: 0,
   64  		output: 0,
   65  		cacheRead: 0,
   66  		cacheWrite: 0,
   67  		totalTokens: 0,
   68  		cost: { input: 0, output: 0, cacheRead: 0, cacheWrite: 0, total: 0 },
   69  	};
   70  }
   71
   72  function addUsageTotals(target: Usage, usage: Partial<Usage>): void {
   73  	const input = usage.input ?? 0;
   74  	const output = usage.output ?? 0;
   75  	const cacheRead = usage.cacheRead ?? 0;
   76  	const cacheWrite = usage.cacheWrite ?? 0;
   77  	const totalTokens = usage.totalTokens ?? input + output + cacheRead + cacheWrite;
   78  	const cost =
   79  		usage.cost ??
   80  		({
   81  			input: 0,
   82  			output: 0,
   83  			cacheRead: 0,
   84  			cacheWrite: 0,
   85  			total: 0,
   86  		} satisfies Usage["cost"]);
   87
   88  	target.input += input;
   89  	target.output += output;
   90  	target.cacheRead += cacheRead;
   91  	target.cacheWrite += cacheWrite;
   92  	target.totalTokens += totalTokens;
   93  	target.cost.input += cost.input;
   94  	target.cost.output += cost.output;
   95  	target.cost.cacheRead += cost.cacheRead;
   96  	target.cost.cacheWrite += cost.cacheWrite;
   97  	target.cost.total += cost.total;
   98  }
   99

  124  /**
02
L1factcollaboration

02 · 递归、并发、预算、闲置 park 与冷恢复都有硬合同

源码事实

默认 maxConcurrency=32、maxRecursionDepth=2、softRequestBudget=200，超过预算提示收尾、1.5x 强制 yield；idle 420 秒后可 park 到磁盘。reviver 从 session_init 恢复 cwd、tools、spawn policy、depth、MCP gate 和 yield requirement，缺失 workspace 时只保留 transcript。

白话解释

工人不会无限生工人，也能在闲置时卸载、之后按原权限复活；恢复时不凭猜测重建能力。

对自研 Harness 的含义

长任务资源治理和 durable collaboration 明显领先多数 CLI Agent。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 4505–4558
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 4559–4614
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

03
L1factcollaboration

03 · 兄弟 Agent 可用 hub 实时协作，不只回传父节点

源码事实

同批多个子 Agent 会收到 hub 协作建议；AgentRegistry 维护 live/idle/parked 实例，hub 支持 list/message/broadcast/wait，主会话与 RPC 也能显示和路由子 Agent 状态。

白话解释

几个工人不必各做各的等老板汇总，可以在工作中互相发消息、广播冲突和等待对方。

对自研 Harness 的含义

减少串行 handoff，但需要冲突检测、消息审计和身份权限。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 412–419
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 420–429
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

  412  /**
  413   * Suggestion — never a rejection — nudging the spawner to coordinate via the
  414   * hub when one call creates ≥2 live siblings and it still holds spawn
  415   * capacity. Returns undefined when there is nothing to coordinate or peer
  416   * messaging is unavailable.
  417   */
  418  export function buildCoordinationAdvisory(
  419  	items: TaskItem[],
  420  	depthCapacity: boolean,
  421  	ircEnabled: boolean,
  422  ): string | undefined {
  423  	if (!depthCapacity || !ircEnabled || items.length < 2) return undefined;
  424  	return (
  425  		`Coordinate: ${items.length} siblings are running together. If their work overlaps, have them ` +
  426  		`message each other via \`hub\` (by id, or "all" to broadcast) before editing shared files — ` +
  427  		`live coordination beats a serial handoff. Check \`hub\` op:"list" to see who is doing what.`
  428  	);
  429  }
  430
  431  /**
  432   * Compose the non-blocking advisory appended to a `task` result: the
  433   * specialization nudge (from the per-item resolved agent types), plus — only
  434   * when some spawns keep running after this call (`willRunAsync`) — the
  435   * coordination suggestion over those still-live spawns (`items`). Coordination
  436   * is gated on async because a sync spawn has already finished by the time the
  437   * call returns, so a "coordinate while they run" hint would misfire. Returns
  438   * undefined when neither applies.
  439   */
  440  export function composeSpawnAdvisory(args: {
  441  	agents: string[];
  442  	items: TaskItem[];
  443  	depthCapacity: boolean;
  444  	ircEnabled: boolean;
  445  	willRunAsync: boolean;
  446  }): string | undefined {
  447  	return (
  448  		[
  449  			buildSpecializationAdvisory(args.agents, args.depthCapacity),
  450  			args.willRunAsync ? buildCoordinationAdvisory(args.items, args.depthCapacity, args.ircEnabled) : undefined,
  451  		]
  452  			.filter(Boolean)
  453  			.join("\n\n") || undefined
  454  	);
  455  }

  457  /** Sentinel for async jobs whose subagent finished with a failing result; progress is already updated. */
  458  class TaskJobError extends Error {}

  460  /**
  461   * Process-level memo for create-time agent discovery, keyed by resolved cwd.
  462   *
  463   * `TaskTool.create` runs for every (sub)agent session in this process and the
  464   * walk-up + plugin-registry scan in `discoverAgents` is identical for a given
  465   * cwd, so repeat creations reuse the first scan. Execution-time discovery
  466   * (`#runSpawn`) intentionally stays fresh. The memo also tracks the live
  467   * `discoverAgents` binding: test spies swap that binding, which invalidates
  468   * the memo automatically.
  469   */
  470  const discoveryMemo = new Map<string, Promise<DiscoveryResult>>();
  471  let discoveryMemoFn: typeof discoverAgents | undefined;
  472
  473  function discoverAgentsForCreate(cwd: string): Promise<DiscoveryResult> {
  474  	const fn = discoverAgents;
  475  	if (discoveryMemoFn !== fn) {
  476  		discoveryMemoFn = fn;
  477  		discoveryMemo.clear();
  478  	}
  479  	const key = path.resolve(cwd);
  480  	let pending = discoveryMemo.get(key);
  481  	if (!pending) {
  482  		pending = fn(cwd);
  483  		discoveryMemo.set(key, pending);
  484  		pending.catch(() => {
  485  			if (discoveryMemo.get(key) === pending) discoveryMemo.delete(key);
  486  		});
  487  	}
  488  	return pending;
  489  }

  500   * disabled, the tool blocks until every spawn finishes.

  412
  413  /**
  414   * Compose the non-blocking advisory appended to a `task` result: the
  415   * specialization nudge (from the per-item resolved agent types), plus — only
  416   * when some spawns keep running after this call (`willRunAsync`) — the
  417   * coordination suggestion over those still-live spawns (`items`). Coordination
  418   * is gated on async because a sync spawn has already finished by the time the
  419   * call returns, so a "coordinate while they run" hint would misfire. Returns
  420   * undefined when neither applies.
  421   */
  422  export function composeSpawnAdvisory(args: {
  423  	agents: string[];
  424  	items: TaskItem[];
  425  	depthCapacity: boolean;
  426  	ircEnabled: boolean;
  427  	willRunAsync: boolean;
  428  	scoutAvailable?: boolean;
  429  }): string | undefined {
  430  	return (
  431  		[
  432  			buildSpecializationAdvisory(args.agents, args.depthCapacity, args.scoutAvailable),
  433  			args.willRunAsync ? buildCoordinationAdvisory(args.items, args.depthCapacity, args.ircEnabled) : undefined,
  434  		]
  435  			.filter(Boolean)
  436  			.join("\n\n") || undefined
  437  	);
  438  }
  439
  440  /** Sentinel for async jobs whose subagent finished with a failing result; progress is already updated. */
  441  class TaskJobError extends Error {}
  442
  443  /**
  444   * Process-level create-time discovery memo and published reload snapshots,
  445   * keyed by resolved cwd.
  446   *
  447   * `TaskTool.create` runs for every (sub)agent session in this process and the
  448   * walk-up + plugin-registry scan in `discoverAgents` is identical for a given
  449   * cwd, so repeat creations reuse the first scan. Explicit plugin reloads
  450   * replace the matching snapshot so already-created tools advertise the latest
  451   * definitions. Execution-time discovery (`#runSpawn`) intentionally stays
  452   * fresh. The memo also tracks the live `discoverAgents` binding: test spies
  453   * swap that binding, which invalidates both caches automatically.
  454   */
  455  const discoveryMemo = new Map<string, Promise<DiscoveryResult>>();
  456  const discoverySnapshots = new Map<string, AgentDefinition[]>();
  457  let discoveryMemoFn: typeof discoverAgents | undefined;
  458
  459  function discoverAgentsForCreate(cwd: string): Promise<DiscoveryResult> {
  460  	const fn = discoverAgents;
  461  	if (discoveryMemoFn !== fn) {
  462  		discoveryMemoFn = fn;
  463  		discoveryMemo.clear();
  464  		discoverySnapshots.clear();
  465  	}
  466  	const key = path.resolve(cwd);
  467  	let pending = discoveryMemo.get(key);
  468  	if (!pending) {
  469  		pending = fn(cwd);
  470  		discoveryMemo.set(key, pending);
  471  		pending.catch(() => {
  472  			if (discoveryMemo.get(key) === pending) discoveryMemo.delete(key);
  473  		});
  474  	}
  475  	return pending;
  476  }
  477
  478  /** Rescan one cwd and publish its definitions to existing and future task tools. */
  479  export async function refreshAgentDiscovery(cwd: string): Promise<void> {
  480  	const key = path.resolve(cwd);
  481  	discoveryMemo.delete(key);
  482  	const pending = discoverAgentsForCreate(cwd);
  483  	const { agents } = await pending;
  484  	if (discoveryMemo.get(key) === pending) {
  485  		discoverySnapshots.set(key, agents);
  486  	}
  487  }

  489  // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  490  // Tool Class
  491  // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

  493  /**
  494   * Task tool - Delegate tasks to specialized agents.
  495   *
  496   * Each call spawns one subagent — or, with `task.batch`, one per `tasks[]`
  497   * item. When `async.enabled` is on, spawns run as AsyncJobManager jobs; when
  498   * disabled, the tool blocks until every spawn finishes.
  499   */
  500  export class TaskTool implements AgentTool<TaskToolSchemaInstance, TaskToolDetails, Theme> {

DIMENSION · INSTRUCTIONS

全部指令与扩展面

2 条可定位结论；每条按事实 → 白话 → 工程影响 → 源码摘录展开。

01
L1factinstructions

01 · 指令层兼容多个 Agent 生态，并支持 @include

源码事实

system builder 组合 personality、tool inventory、环境、workspace tree、active repo、project rules、skills、goals/memory/plan/task guidance；context capability 从父目录到 cwd 加载 AGENTS/CLAUDE/GEMINI 等，近目录后置，并展开相对 @path imports、去重同内容。

白话解释

它会把多种 Agent 留在仓库里的说明书都读成统一规则，越靠近当前目录的规则越显眼，还能在规则里引用其他文件。

对自研 Harness 的含义

跨工具迁移成本低，但最终 prompt provenance 和冲突诊断至关重要。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 332–367
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 368–405
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

叙述来自历史证据账本 · ed4c78bc0faa · 打开账本提交 ↗
packages/coding-agent/src/system-prompt.ts:332–405固定提交 ↗
  332  export interface LoadContextFilesOptions {
  333  	/** Working directory to start walking up from. Default: getProjectDir() */
  334  	cwd?: string;
  335  }
  336
  337  function dedupeExactContextFiles(
  338  	contextFiles: Array<{ path: string; content: string; depth?: number }>,
  339  ): Array<{ path: string; content: string; depth?: number }> {
  340  	const lastIndexByContent = new Map<string, number>();
  341  	for (const [index, file] of contextFiles.entries()) {
  342  		// Keep the closest matching context entry when content is byte-for-byte identical.
  343  		lastIndexByContent.set(file.content, index);
  344  	}
  345
  346  	return contextFiles.filter((file, index) => lastIndexByContent.get(file.content) === index);
  347  }
  348
  349  /**
  350   * Load all project context files using the capability API.
  351   * Returns {path, content, depth} entries for all discovered context files.
  352   * Files are sorted by depth (descending) so files closer to cwd appear last/more prominent.
  353   */
  354  export async function loadProjectContextFiles(
  355  	options: LoadContextFilesOptions = {},
  356  ): Promise<Array<{ path: string; content: string; depth?: number }>> {
  357  	const resolvedCwd = options.cwd ?? getProjectDir();
  358
  359  	const result = await loadCapability(contextFileCapability.id, { cwd: resolvedCwd });
  360
  361  	// Materialize ContextFile items, expanding any `@path/to/file` includes
  362  	// in their content. The expansion uses the file's own directory as the
  363  	// resolution base so relative imports work the same way Claude Code,
  364  	// Goose, and other tools document.
  365  	const files = await Promise.all(
  366  		result.items.map(async item => {
  367  			const contextFile = item as ContextFile;
  368  			return {
  369  				path: contextFile.path,
  370  				content: await expandAtImports(contextFile.content, contextFile.path),
  371  				depth: contextFile.depth,
  372  			};
  373  		}),
  374  	);
  375
  376  	// Sort by depth (descending): higher depth (farther from cwd) comes first,
  377  	// so files closer to cwd appear later and are more prominent
  378  	files.sort((a, b) => {
  379  		const depthA = a.depth ?? -1;
  380  		const depthB = b.depth ?? -1;
  381  		return depthB - depthA;
  382  	});
  383
  384  	return dedupeExactContextFiles(files);
  385  }
  386
  387  /**
  388   * Load the effective system prompt customization from SYSTEM.md.
  389   * Project-level SYSTEM.md overrides user-level SYSTEM.md.
  390   */
  391  export async function loadSystemPromptFiles(options: LoadContextFilesOptions = {}): Promise<string | null> {
  392  	const resolvedCwd = options.cwd ?? getProjectDir();
  393
  394  	const result = await loadCapability<SystemPromptFile>(systemPromptCapability.id, { cwd: resolvedCwd });
  395
  396  	if (result.items.length === 0) return null;
  397
  398  	const projectLevel = result.items.find(item => item.level === "project");
  399  	if (projectLevel) {
  400  		return projectLevel.content;
  401  	}
  402
  403  	const userLevel = result.items.find(item => item.level === "user");
  404  	return userLevel?.content ?? null;
  405  }

  407  export const DEFAULT_SYSTEM_PROMPT_TOOL_NAMES = ["read", "bash", "edit", "write"] as const;
  408
  409  export interface SystemPromptToolMetadata {
  410  	label: string;
  411  	description: string;
  412  	/** Tool name the model sees on the provider wire. Defaults to the internal tool name. */
  413  	wireName?: string;
  414  	/** Tool parameters schema (Zod or JSON Schema), fed to the verbose inventory renderer. */
  415  	parameters?: TSchema;
  416  	/** Illustrative examples rendered into the verbose inventory. */
  417  	examples?: readonly ToolExample[];
  418  }
  419
  420  export type SystemPromptToolMetadataProjection =
  421  	| {
  422  			mode: "compact";
  423  			toolNames: readonly string[];
  424  			overrides?: Partial<Record<string, Partial<SystemPromptToolMetadata>>>;
  425  	  }
  426  	| {
  427  			mode: "full";
  428  			overrides?: Partial<Record<string, Partial<SystemPromptToolMetadata>>>;
  429  	  };
  430
  431  export function buildSystemPromptToolMetadata(
  432  	tools: Map<string, AgentTool>,
  433  	overrides: Partial<Record<string, Partial<SystemPromptToolMetadata>>> = {},
  434  ): Map<string, SystemPromptToolMetadata> {
  435  	return projectSystemPromptToolMetadata(tools, { mode: "full", overrides });
  436  }
  437
  438  /** Builds a mode-specific metadata snapshot for internal prompt assembly. */
  439  export function projectSystemPromptToolMetadata(
  440  	tools: Map<string, AgentTool>,
  441  	projection: SystemPromptToolMetadataProjection,
  442  ): Map<string, SystemPromptToolMetadata> {
  443  	const metadata = new Map<string, SystemPromptToolMetadata>();
  444  	const addTool = (name: string, tool: AgentTool): void => {
  445  		const override = projection.overrides?.[name];
  446  		const labelValue = override?.label ?? tool.label;
  447  		const wireNameValue = override?.wireName ?? tool.customWireName;
  448  		const label = typeof labelValue === "string" ? labelValue : "";
  449  		const wireName = typeof wireNameValue === "string" ? wireNameValue : undefined;
  450
  451  		if (projection.mode === "compact") {
  452  			metadata.set(name, { label, description: "", wireName });
  453  			return;
  454  		}
  455
  456  		const descriptionValue = override?.description ?? tool.description;
  457  		metadata.set(name, {
  458  			label,
  459  			description: typeof descriptionValue === "string" ? descriptionValue : "",
  460  			parameters: tool.parameters,
  461  			examples: tool.examples,
  462  			wireName,
  463  		});
  464  	};
  465
  466  	if (projection.mode === "compact") {
  467  		for (const name of projection.toolNames) {
  468  			const tool = tools.get(name);
  469  			if (tool) addTool(name, tool);
  470  		}
  471  	} else {
  472  		for (const [name, tool] of tools) addTool(name, tool);
  473  	}
  474
  475  	return metadata;
  476  }
