  332  export interface LoadContextFilesOptions {
  333  	/** Working directory to start walking up from. Default: getProjectDir() */
  334  	cwd?: string;
  335  	/** Disabled extension IDs to honor instead of the process-global settings. */
  336  	disabledExtensions?: string[];
  337  }
  338
  339  function dedupeExactContextFiles(
  340  	contextFiles: Array<{ path: string; content: string; depth?: number }>,
  341  ): Array<{ path: string; content: string; depth?: number }> {
  342  	const lastIndexByContent = new Map<string, number>();
  343  	for (const [index, file] of contextFiles.entries()) {
  344  		// Keep the closest matching context entry when content is byte-for-byte identical.
  345  		lastIndexByContent.set(file.content, index);
  346  	}
  347
  348  	return contextFiles.filter((file, index) => lastIndexByContent.get(file.content) === index);
  349  }
  350
  351  /**
  352   * Load all project context files using the capability API.
  353   * Returns {path, content, depth} entries for all discovered context files.
  354   * Files are sorted by depth (descending) so files closer to cwd appear last/more prominent.
  355   */
  356  export async function loadProjectContextFiles(
  357  	options: LoadContextFilesOptions = {},
  358  ): Promise<Array<{ path: string; content: string; depth?: number }>> {
  359  	const resolvedCwd = options.cwd ?? getProjectDir();
  360
  361  	const result = await loadCapability(contextFileCapability.id, {
  362  		cwd: resolvedCwd,
  363  		disabledExtensions: options.disabledExtensions,
  364  	});
  365
  366  	// Materialize ContextFile items, expanding any `@path/to/file` includes
  367  	// in their content. The expansion uses the file's own directory as the
  368  	// resolution base so relative imports work the same way Claude Code,
  369  	// Goose, and other tools document.
  370  	const files = await Promise.all(
  371  		result.items.map(async item => {
  372  			const contextFile = item as ContextFile;
  373  			return {
  374  				path: contextFile.path,
  375  				content: await expandAtImports(contextFile.content, contextFile.path),
  376  				depth: contextFile.depth,
  377  			};
  378  		}),
  379  	);
  380
  381  	// Sort by depth (descending): higher depth (farther from cwd) comes first,
  382  	// so files closer to cwd appear later and are more prominent
  383  	files.sort((a, b) => {
  384  		const depthA = a.depth ?? -1;
  385  		const depthB = b.depth ?? -1;
  386  		return depthB - depthA;
  387  	});
  388
  389  	return dedupeExactContextFiles(files);
  390  }

02
L1riskinstructions

02 · Extensions、hooks、custom tools 与 marketplace 都是同进程高权限扩展

源码事实

extension loader 直接加载 JS/TS，API 可改 provider request、system/context、tool call/result、session lifecycle 并注册 tools/providers/UI；hooks 有独立 runner，plugins 可从 npm/git/marketplace 安装并兼容 legacy Pi/Claude manifests。

白话解释

插件几乎能改 Agent 的每个关节，也能执行宿主代码；插件市场因此既是生态优势，也是供应链入口。

对自研 Harness 的含义

企业需要签名、来源锁定、版本 pin、审批和最小权限进程边界。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 120–174
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 175–230
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

  146  /**
  147   * ExtensionAPI implementation for an extension.
  148   * Registration methods write to the extension object.
  149   * Action methods delegate to the shared runtime.
  150   */
  151  class ConcreteExtensionAPI implements ExtensionAPI, IExtensionRuntime {
  152  	readonly logger = logger;
  153  	readonly typebox = TypeBox;
  154  	readonly arktype = type;
  155  	readonly zod = zod;
  156  	readonly flagValues = new Map<string, boolean | string>();
  157  	readonly pendingProviderRegistrations: Array<{
  158  		name: string;
  159  		config: ProviderConfig;
  160  		sourceId: string;
  161  	}> = [];

  164  		public readonly pi: typeof PiCodingAgent,
  165  		private readonly extension: Extension,
  166  		private readonly runtime: IExtensionRuntime,
  167  		private readonly cwd: string,
  168  		public readonly events: EventBus,
  169  	) {}
  170
  171  	on<F extends HandlerFn>(event: string, handler: F): void {
  172  		const list = this.extension.handlers.get(event) ?? [];
  173  		list.push(handler);
  174  		this.extension.handlers.set(event, list);
  175  	}
  176
  177  	registerTool<TParams extends TSchema = TSchema, TDetails = unknown>(tool: ToolDefinition<TParams, TDetails>): void {
  178  		const registered = {
  179  			definition: tool,
  180  			extensionPath: this.extension.path,
  181  		};
  182  		this.extension.tools.set(tool.name, registered);
  183  		for (const listener of this.extension.toolRegistrationListeners ?? []) listener(tool.name);
  184  	}
  185
  186  	registerCommand(
  187  		name: string,
  188  		options: {
  189  			description?: string;
  190  			getArgumentCompletions?: RegisteredCommand["getArgumentCompletions"];
  191  			handler: RegisteredCommand["handler"];
  192  		},
  193  	): void {
  194  		this.extension.commands.set(name, { name, ...options });
  195  	}
  196
  197  	setLabel(label: string): void {

  199  	}

  206  		},
  207  	): void {
  208  		this.extension.shortcuts.set(shortcut, { shortcut, extensionPath: this.extension.path, ...options });
  209  	}
  210
  211  	registerFlag(
  212  		name: string,
  213  		options: { description?: string; type: "boolean" | "string"; default?: boolean | string },
  214  	): void {
  215  		this.extension.flags.set(name, { name, extensionPath: this.extension.path, ...options });
  216  		if (options.default !== undefined) {
  217  			this.runtime.flagValues.set(name, options.default);
  218  		}
  219  	}

  223  	}
  224
  225  	registerAssistantThinkingRenderer(renderer: AssistantThinkingRenderer): void {
  226  		this.extension.assistantThinkingRenderers.push(renderer);

  229  	getFlag(name: string): boolean | string | undefined {
  230  		if (!this.extension.flags.has(name)) return undefined;

  232  	}

11
DIMENSION · RECOVERY

恢复、测试与取舍

1 条可定位结论；每条按事实 → 白话 → 工程影响 → 源码摘录展开。

01
L3factrecovery

01 · 机制测试和基准工程极密集，但仍需外部成功率验证

源码事实

固定提交含 1858 个 *.test.ts，其中 coding-agent 1163 个，覆盖 MCP reconnect/OAuth/transport、task isolation/revive/budget、compaction、providers、tools、UI 和 storage；另有 metaharness、TypeScript edit benchmark、native/hashline/memory benchmarks。

白话解释

零件级和故障恢复考试数量非常大，也有专门实验 Harness；但仓库内测试多不代表在 SWE-bench 或真实企业任务上一定胜出。

对自研 Harness 的含义

工程可靠性证据强；横向结论应把“机制覆盖”与“端到端任务成功率”分开。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 1–49
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 50–100
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

   38  async function git(repoRoot: string, ...args: string[]): Promise<string> {
   39  	const result = await $`git ${args}`.cwd(repoRoot).quiet().nothrow();
   40  	if (result.exitCode !== 0) {
   41  		throw new Error(`git ${args.join(" ")} failed: ${result.stderr.toString()}`);
   42  	}
   43  	return result.text();
   44  }

   46  async function seedFooRepo(finalContent: string): Promise<{ repoRoot: string; patchPath: string }> {
   47  	const repoRoot = await fs.mkdtemp(path.join(os.tmpdir(), "omp-isolation-merge-"));
   48  	tempRoots.push(repoRoot);
   49
   50  	await git(repoRoot, "init");
   51  	await git(repoRoot, "config", "user.email", "repro@example.com");
   52  	await git(repoRoot, "config", "user.name", "Repro");
   53  	await Bun.write(path.join(repoRoot, "foo.txt"), "old\n");
   54  	await git(repoRoot, "add", "foo.txt");
   55  	await git(repoRoot, "commit", "-m", "base");
   56  	await Bun.write(path.join(repoRoot, "foo.txt"), "new\n");
   57  	await git(repoRoot, "commit", "-am", "change to new");
   58
   59  	const patchPath = path.join(repoRoot, "task.patch");
   60  	const patchText = await git(repoRoot, "diff-tree", "--binary", "--full-index", "--no-commit-id", "-p", "HEAD");
   61  	await Bun.write(patchPath, patchText);
   62
   63  	if (finalContent !== "new\n") {
   64  		await git(repoRoot, "reset", "--hard", "HEAD~1");
   65  		if (finalContent !== "old\n") {
   66  			await Bun.write(path.join(repoRoot, "foo.txt"), finalContent);
   67  			await git(repoRoot, "commit", "-am", "diverge");
   68  		}
   69  	}
   70  	return { repoRoot, patchPath };
   71  }

   52  	await git(repoRoot, "config", "user.email", "repro@example.com");
   53  	await git(repoRoot, "config", "user.name", "Repro");
   54  	await Bun.write(path.join(repoRoot, "foo.txt"), "old\n");
   55  	await git(repoRoot, "add", "foo.txt");
   56  	await git(repoRoot, "commit", "-m", "base");
   57  	await Bun.write(path.join(repoRoot, "foo.txt"), "new\n");
   58  	await git(repoRoot, "commit", "-am", "change to new");
   59
   60  	const patchPath = path.join(repoRoot, "task.patch");
   61  	const patchText = await git(repoRoot, "diff-tree", "--binary", "--full-index", "--no-commit-id", "-p", "HEAD");
   62  	await Bun.write(patchPath, patchText);
   63
   64  	if (finalContent !== "new\n") {
   65  		await git(repoRoot, "reset", "--hard", "HEAD~1");
   66  		if (finalContent !== "old\n") {
   67  			await Bun.write(path.join(repoRoot, "foo.txt"), finalContent);
   68  			await git(repoRoot, "commit", "-am", "diverge");
   69  		}
   70  	}
   71  	return { repoRoot, patchPath };
   72  }
   73
   74  describe("runIsolatedSubprocess", () => {
   75  	afterEach(async () => {
   76  		vi.restoreAllMocks();
   77  		AgentRegistry.resetGlobalForTests();
   78  		await Promise.all(tempRoots.splice(0).map(tempRoot => fs.rm(tempRoot, { force: true, recursive: true })));
   79  	});
   80
   81  	it("preserves branch-mode output as a patch when branch transfer fails", async () => {
   82  		const repoRoot = await fs.mkdtemp(path.join(os.tmpdir(), "omp-isolation-run-"));
   83  		tempRoots.push(repoRoot);
   84  		const isolationDir = path.join(repoRoot, "isolated");
   85  		const artifactsDir = path.join(repoRoot, "artifacts");
   86  		const baseline = {
   87  			root: {
   88  				repoRoot,
   89  				headCommit: "base",
   90  				staged: "",
   91  				unstaged: "",
   92  				untracked: [],
   93  				untrackedPatch: "",
   94  			},
   95  			nested: [],
   96  		};
   97  		const rootPatch = "diff --git a/task.txt b/task.txt\n--- a/task.txt\n+++ b/task.txt\n@@ -1 +1 @@\n-old\n+new\n";
   98
   99  		vi.spyOn(worktreeModule, "ensureIsolation").mockResolvedValue({
  100  			mergedDir: isolationDir,
  101  			backend: natives.IsoBackendKind.Rcopy,
  102  			fellBack: false,
  103  			fallbackReason: null,
  104  		});
  105  		vi.spyOn(executorModule, "runSubprocess").mockResolvedValue(result({ id: "PreserveBranchFailure" }));
  106  		vi.spyOn(worktreeModule, "commitToBranch").mockRejectedValue(new Error("remote: object corrupt"));
  107  		const captureSpy = vi.spyOn(worktreeModule, "captureDeltaPatch").mockResolvedValue({
  108  			rootPatch,
  109  			nestedPatches: [],
  110  		});
  111  		const cleanupSpy = vi.spyOn(worktreeModule, "cleanupIsolation").mockResolvedValue();
  112  		AgentRegistry.global().register({
  113  			id: "PreserveBranchFailure",
  114  			displayName: "PreserveBranchFailure",
  115  			kind: "sub",
  116  			session: null,
  117  			status: "parked",
  118  		});
  119  		const deleteSpy = vi.spyOn(gitModule.branch, "tryDelete").mockResolvedValue(true);
  120
  121  		const outcome = await runIsolatedSubprocess({
  122  			baseOptions: {
  123  				cwd: repoRoot,
  124  				agent: {
  125  					name: "task",
  126  					description: "Task agent",
  127  					systemPrompt: "test",
  128  					source: "bundled",
  129  				},
  130  				task: "Do work",
  131  				index: 0,
  132  				id: "PreserveBranchFailure",
  133  			},
  134  			context: { repoRoot, baseline },
  135  			preferredBackend: undefined,
  136  			agentId: "PreserveBranchFailure",
  137  			mergeMode: "branch",
  138  			artifactsDir,
  139  			buildFailureResult: err => result({ exitCode: 1, error: String(err) }),
  140  		});
  141
  142  		const patchPath = path.join(artifactsDir, "PreserveBranchFailure.patch");
  143  		expect(outcome.error).toContain("Merge failed: remote: object corrupt");
  144  		expect(outcome.patchPath).toBe(patchPath);
  145  		expect(await Bun.file(patchPath).text()).toBe(rootPatch);
  146  		expect(outcome.nestedPatches).toEqual([]);
  147  		expect(captureSpy).toHaveBeenCalledWith(isolationDir, baseline);
  148  		expect(deleteSpy).toHaveBeenCalledWith(repoRoot, "omp/task/PreserveBranchFailure");
  149  		expect(cleanupSpy).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
  150  		expect(AgentRegistry.global().get("PreserveBranchFailure")?.history?.patchPath).toBe(patchPath);
  151  	});
  152
  153  	it("keeps an isolated worktree until deferred child cleanup settles", async () => {
  154  		const cleanupGate = Promise.withResolvers<void>();
  155  		vi.spyOn(worktreeModule, "ensureIsolation").mockResolvedValue({
  156  			mergedDir: "/repo/isolated",
  157  			backend: natives.IsoBackendKind.Rcopy,
  158  			fellBack: false,
  159  			fallbackReason: null,
  160  		});
  161  		vi.spyOn(executorModule, "runSubprocess").mockImplementation(async options => {
  162  			options.onCleanupDeferred?.(cleanupGate.promise);
  163  			return result({ exitCode: 1, aborted: true, error: "cleanup exceeded its deadline" });
  164  		});
  165  		const cleanupSpy = vi.spyOn(worktreeModule, "cleanupIsolation").mockResolvedValue();
  166
  167  		const outcome = await runIsolatedSubprocess({
  168  			baseOptions: {
  169  				cwd: "/repo",
  170  				agent: {
  171  					name: "task",
